\Pr\left[\left|\frac{X}{d_{1}}-1\right|\geq\varepsilon\right]\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{d_{1}\varepsilon^{2}}{4}\right).

(224)

Therefore, we have:

\Pr\left[\left|\frac{\left\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\right\|_{2}^{2}}{\sigma_{0}^{2}d_{1}}-1\right|\geq\varepsilon\right]\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{d_{1}\varepsilon^{2}}{4}\right).

(225)

Choose a suitable
\varepsilon
to derive the precision range and we choose:
\varepsilon=\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{1}}}\right)
.

At this time, the probability of deviation is:

\Pr\left[\left|\left\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\sigma_{0}^{2}d_{1}\right|\leq\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0}^{2}\sqrt{d_{1}})\right]\geq 1-\frac{1}{\operatorname{poly}(d)}.

(226)

That is:

\left\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\right\|_{2}^{2}\in\left[\sigma_{0}^{2}d_{1}\left(1-\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{1}}}\right)\right),\,\sigma_{0}^{2}d_{1}\left(1+\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{1}}}\right)\right)\right].

(227)

This holds with high probability (
1-\frac{1}{\operatorname{poly}(d)}
).
∎

G.2
Proof of Lemma
B.2
(b):

Proof of Lemma
B.2
(b):

Let:

\bm{Z}_{i}:=\frac{1}{\sigma_{0}}\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\bm{I}_{d_{1}}).

(228)

Then we have:

\left\|\bm{MM}^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\right\|_{2}^{2}=\sigma_{0}^{2}\cdot\left\|\bm{MM}^{\top}\bm{Z}_{i}\right\|_{2}^{2}.

(229)

We regard
\bm{MM}^{\top}
as a rank-
d
projection matrix, projecting
\bm{Z}_{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{1}}
onto the column space of
\bm{M}
so we can use the following property:

If
\bm{MM}^{\top}
is a fixed rank-
d
projection matrix, and
\bm{Z}_{i}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\bm{I}_{d_{1}})
, then:

\|\bm{MM}^{\top}\bm{Z}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}=\bm{Z}_{i}^{\top}(\bm{MM}^{\top})^{\top}\bm{MM}^{\top}\bm{Z}_{i}=\bm{Z}_{i}^{\top}\bm{MM}^{\top}\bm{Z}_{i}=\|\bm{M}^{\top}\bm{Z}_{i}\|_{2}^{2},

(230)

\displaystyle\bm{M}^{\top}\bm{Z}_{i}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,I_{d}).

(231)

Therefore, we can conclude:

\left\|\bm{MM}^{\top}\bm{Z}_{i}\right\|_{2}^{2}\sim\chi^{2}(d)\quad\Longrightarrow\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\bm{MM}^{\top}\bm{Z}_{i}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]=d.

(232)

And it satisfies the following Chi-square concentration inequality:

\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\left\|\bm{MM}^{\top}\bm{Z}_{i}\right\|_{2}^{2}-d\right|\leq\varepsilon d\right)\geq 1-2\exp\left(-c\varepsilon^{2}d\right).

(233)

Choose
\varepsilon=\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/\sqrt{d})
, and the result holds with high probability. We substitute back
\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}

\left\|\bm{M}\bm{M}^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\right\|_{2}^{2}=\sigma_{0}^{2}\cdot\left\|\bm{M}\bm{M}^{\top}\bm{Z}_{i}\right\|_{2}^{2}\in\left[\sigma_{0}^{2}d\left(1-\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}}\right)\right),\sigma_{0}^{2}d\left(1+\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}}\right)\right)\right].

(234)

∎

G.3
Proof of Lemma
B.2
(c):

Proof of Lemma
B.2
(c):

Recall if
g
is standard Gaussian, then for every
t>0
,

\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\cdot\frac{t}{t^{2}+1}e^{-t^{2}/2}<\Pr_{g\sim\mathcal{N}(0,1)}\left[g>t\right]<\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\cdot\frac{1}{t}e^{-t^{2}/2}.

(235)

Therefore, for every
i\in[m]
and
j\in[d]
,

\displaystyle p_{1}

\displaystyle=\Pr\left[\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle^{2}\geq\frac{c_{1}\log d}{d}\|\bm{MM}^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\|_{2}^{2}\right]

(236)

\displaystyle=\Pr\left[\frac{\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle}{\sigma_{0}}\geq\sqrt{c_{1}\log d}\right]

\displaystyle\geq\Omega\left(\frac{1}{d^{c_{1}/2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\Omega\left(\frac{1}{d^{(\frac{\epsilon_{\max}}{\epsilon_{\min}})^{2}\cdot(1+\gamma)}}\right),

and

\displaystyle p_{2}

\displaystyle=\Pr\left[\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle^{2}\geq\frac{c_{2}\log d}{d}\|\bm{MM}^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\|_{2}^{2}\right]

(237)

\displaystyle=\Pr\left[\frac{\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle}{\sigma_{0}}\geq\sqrt{c_{2}\log d}\right]

\displaystyle\leq O\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\log d}}\right)\cdot\frac{1}{d^{c_{2}/2}}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\log d}}\right)\cdot\frac{1}{d^{(\frac{\epsilon_{\min}}{\epsilon_{\max}})^{2}\cdot(1-\gamma)}}.

We define the following events in definition
B.1
:

•

A_{i}
: Lucky neuron
i
satisfies conditions 1(i.e., the response is large enough and in the correct direction)

•

B_{i}
: for all
j^{\prime}\neq j
, lucky neuron
i
satisfies condition 2 (i.e., small responses in other directions)

We now compute the probability of the intersection event
A_{i}\cap B_{i}
:

\displaystyle\Pr[A_{i}]

\displaystyle=\frac{p_{1}}{2}=\Omega\left(d^{-\left(\frac{\epsilon_{\max}}{\epsilon_{\min}}\right)^{2}\cdot(1+\gamma)}\right),

(238)

\displaystyle\Pr[B_{i}]

\displaystyle=(1-p_{2})^{d-1}=e^{-(d-1)p_{2}}=e^{-(d-1)d^{-a}}=e^{-d^{1-a}}=1,

\displaystyle\Pr[A_{i}\cap B_{i}]

\displaystyle=\frac{p_{1}}{2}\cdot(1-p_{2})^{d-1}=\Omega\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\log d}}\cdot d^{-\left(\frac{\epsilon_{\max}}{\epsilon_{\min}}\right)^{2}\cdot(1+\gamma)}\right).

(1) We now have
m=d^{C_{m}}
neurons. Therefore, the expected number is:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[|\mathcal{M}_{j}^{\star}|\right]

\displaystyle=m\cdot\Pr[A_{i}\cap B_{i}]=d^{C_{m}}\cdot\Omega\left(d^{-\left(\frac{\epsilon_{\max}}{\epsilon_{\min}}\right)^{2}\cdot(1+\gamma)}\right)

(239)

\displaystyle=\Omega\left(d^{C_{m}-\left(\frac{\epsilon_{\max}}{\epsilon_{\min}}\right)^{2}\cdot(1+\gamma)}\right).

Chernoff bound (Lower-tail form): For any
\delta\in(0,1)
, we have:

\Pr\left[\sum X_{i}<(1-\delta)\mu\right]\leq e^{-\frac{\delta^{2}}{2}\mu}.

(240)

Let
\delta=\frac{1}{2}
, we obtain:

\displaystyle\Pr\left[\sum X_{i}<\frac{1}{2}\mu\right]

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\mu/8}

(241)

\displaystyle\Pr\left[|\mathcal{M}_{j}^{\star}|<O\left(d^{\omega_{1}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\Omega\left(d^{\omega_{1}}\right)}

\displaystyle\Pr\left[|\mathcal{M}_{j}^{\star}|>\Omega\left(d^{\omega_{1}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\geq 1-e^{-\Omega\left(d^{\omega_{1}}\right)}.

(2) We now have
m=d^{C_{m}}
neurons. Therefore, the expected number is:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\mathcal{M}_{j}\right|\right]

\displaystyle=m\cdot p_{2}=d^{C_{m}}\cdot\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\log d}}\cdot d^{-\left(\frac{\epsilon_{\min}}{\epsilon_{\max}}\right)^{2}\cdot(1-\gamma)}\right)

(242)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\log d}}\cdot d^{C_{m}-\left(\frac{\epsilon_{\min}}{\epsilon_{\max}}\right)^{2}\cdot(1-\gamma)}\right).

Chernoff bound (upper tail) tells us that for any
0<\delta<1
, we have:

\displaystyle\Pr\left[\sum X_{i}>(1+\delta)\mu\right]

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\Omega(\delta^{2}\mu)}

(243)

\displaystyle\Pr\left[\left|\mathcal{M}_{j}\right|>\Omega(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\log d}}d^{\omega_{2}})\right]

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\Omega(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\log d}}d^{\omega_{2}})}=o\left(\frac{1}{d^{4}}\right)

\displaystyle\Pr\left[\left|\mathcal{M}_{j}\right|<O\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\log d}}d^{\omega_{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\geq 1-o\left(\frac{1}{d^{4}}\right)

\displaystyle\Pr\left[\left|\mathcal{M}_{j}\right|<O\left(d^{\omega_{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\geq 1-o\left(\frac{1}{d^{4}}\right).

∎

G.4
Proof of Lemma
B.2
(d):

Proof of Lemma
B.2
(d):

We know:
|\mathcal{M}_{j}|\leq O(d^{\omega_{2}})
. There are
d
indices
j\in[d]
. Therefore, the total number of pairs
(i,j)
such that
i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}
is at most:

\sum_{j=1}^{d}|\mathcal{M}_{j}|\leq d\cdot O(d^{\omega_{2}})=O(d^{1+\omega_{2}}).

(244)

On the other hand, the total number of neurons is
m=d^{C_{m}}
. So for any fixed
i
, we define:

\mathcal{N}_{i}:=\{j\in[d]:i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}\}.

(245)

Then,

\sum_{i=1}^{m}|N_{i}|=\sum_{j=1}^{d}|\mathcal{M}_{j}|\leq O(d^{1+\omega_{2}}).

(246)

Therefore,

\mathbb{E}[|N_{i}|]=\frac{1}{m}\sum_{i=1}^{m}|N_{i}|\leq O\!\left(d^{1+\omega_{2}-C_{m}}\right)=O\left(d^{1-\left(\frac{\epsilon_{\min}}{\epsilon_{\max}}\right)^{2}\cdot(1-\gamma)}\right).

(247)

Then:

\displaystyle\Pr\left[\left|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\right|\geq\Omega(\sigma_{0}\log^{1/4}d)\right]\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{t^{2}}{2\sigma_{0}^{2}}\right)=2^{-\Omega(\sqrt{\log d})}.

(248)

Fix
i\in[m]
, and consider
d
different
j
. Each has probability
2^{-\Omega(\sqrt{\log d})}
to exceed the threshold. Therefore, the expectation is:

\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\left\{j\in[d]\;\middle|\;\left|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\right|\geq\Omega\left(\sigma_{0}\log^{1/4}d\right)\right\}\right|\right]=O\left(2^{-\sqrt{\log d}}\cdot d\right).

(249)

∎

Appendix H
Proof of Lemmas in Appendix C

H.1
Useful Lemmas

Lemma H.1

(Logits near initialization)
.

Let
\bm{w}_{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{1}}
for each
i\in[m]
. Suppose

\sum_{i\in[m]}\left\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\right\|_{2}^{2}\leq o\!\left(\tfrac{\tau}{d}\right).

(250)

Then, with high probability over the randomness of
\bm{X}_{n},\bm{Y}_{n},
and
\mathfrak{N}
, it holds that

\left|\ell^{\prime}_{p,t}(\bm{X}_{n},\mathfrak{B})-\tfrac{1}{|\mathfrak{B}|}\right|\cdot\left|\ell^{\prime}_{s,t}(\bm{X}_{n},\mathfrak{B})-\tfrac{1}{|\mathfrak{B}|}\right|\leq\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\!\left(\frac{\sum_{i\in[m]}\left\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\right\|_{2}^{2}}{\tau|\mathfrak{B}|}\right).

(251)

H.2
Proof of Lemma
C.1
:

Proof of Lemma
C.1
:

First, we must determine the precise gradient expression for each feature
\bm{M}_{j}
and
\bm{M}_{j}^{\perp}
. The gradient descent update for the projection of
\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}
onto
\bm{M}_{j}
can be written as

\displaystyle\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle

(252)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle-\eta\langle\nabla_{\bm{w}_{i}}L_{\mathrm{aug}}(f_{t}),\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\;\;\pm\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{\textcircled{\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{1}}}}}{=}

\displaystyle(1-\eta\lambda)\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle+\eta\,\mathbb{E}_{\bm{X}_{n},\bm{Y}_{n}}\!\left[(1-\ell^{\prime}_{p,t}(\bm{X}_{n},\mathfrak{B}))\cdot h_{i,t}(\bm{Y}_{n})\,\langle\nabla_{\bm{w}_{i}}h_{i}(\bm{X}_{n}),\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\right]

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\eta\sum_{\bm{X}_{n,s}\in\mathfrak{N}}\mathbb{E}\!\left[\ell^{\prime}_{s,t}(\bm{X}_{n},\mathfrak{B})\,h_{i,t}(\bm{X}_{n,s})\,\langle\nabla_{\bm{w}_{i}}h(\bm{X}_{n}),\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\right]\;\;\pm\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}.

\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{1}}⃝
is because
\lambda
is the coefficient of the regularization term, as well as the gradient formula obtained earlier.
\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}
is due to the approximation between population gradients and empirical gradients.

For the positive term: we can use Lemma
F.3
and Lemma
H.1
to obtain that:

\mathbb{E}\!\left[(1-\ell^{\prime}_{p,t}(\bm{X}_{n},\mathfrak{B}))\cdot h_{i,t}(\bm{Y}_{n})\,\langle\nabla_{\bm{w}_{i}}h_{i,t}(\bm{X}_{n}),\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\right]=\frac{1}{L^{2}}\,\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\,\mathbb{E}\!\left[\hat{z}_{n,j}^{+}\hat{z}_{n,j}\right].

(253)

For the negative term: Here, the bound needs to be verified because Lemma
H.1
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\!\left[\sum_{X\in\mathfrak{N}}\ell^{\prime}_{s,t}\,h_{i,t}(X)\langle\nabla_{\bm{w}_{i}}h(\bm{X}_{n}),\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\right]

(254)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{X\in\mathfrak{N}}\mathbb{E}\!\left[\left(\ell^{\prime}_{s,t}-\tfrac{1}{|\mathfrak{B}|}\right)h_{i,t}(X)\langle\nabla_{\bm{w}_{i}}h(\bm{X}_{n}),\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\text{\textcircled{\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{1}}}}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\sum_{X\in\mathfrak{N}}\mathbb{E}\!\left[\left|\ell^{\prime}_{s,t}-\tfrac{1}{|\mathfrak{B}|}\right|\cdot|h_{i,t}(X)|\cdot\big|\langle\nabla_{\bm{w}_{i}}h(\bm{X}_{n}),\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\big|\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\text{\textcircled{\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{2}}}}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\widetilde{O}\!\left(\frac{\sum_{i\in[m]}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}}{\tau d}\cdot\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}\right).

\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{1}}⃝
is because The product of each term is less than the product of their absolute values.
\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{2}}⃝
is applied Lemma
H.1
to
\lvert\left(\ell^{\prime}_{s,t}-\frac{1}{|\mathfrak{B}|}\right)\rvert
, Lemma
E.7
to
h_{i,t}(X)
and
|\langle\nabla_{\bm{w}_{i}}h_{i,t}(\bm{X}_{n}),\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|=|\langle\bm{M}_{j},\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|
.

Putting all the above calculations together, we have

\displaystyle\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle

\displaystyle=\left(1-\eta\lambda+\epsilon_{j}\,\frac{\eta C_{z}\log\log d}{d}\right)\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle

(255)

\displaystyle\pm\;\widetilde{O}\!\left(\frac{\eta\sum_{i\in[m]}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}}{\tau d}\cdot\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}\right)\;\pm\;\widetilde{O}\!\left(\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}\right).

Prior to the induction step, we establish, by a similar method, the stochastic gradient descent update of
\bm{w}_{i}
along the dense feature direction
\bm{M}_{j}^{\perp}
. Specifically, we obtain the following update equation:

\displaystyle\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)},\bm{M}_{j}^{\perp}\rangle

(256)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}^{\perp}\rangle-\eta\langle\nabla_{\bm{w}_{i}}L_{\mathrm{aug}}(f_{t}),\bm{M}_{j}^{\perp}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-\eta\lambda)\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}^{\perp}\rangle+\eta\,\mathbb{E}\!\left[(1-\ell^{\prime}_{p,t})\,h_{i,t}(\bm{Y}_{n})\langle\nabla_{\bm{w}_{i}}h_{i,t}(\bm{X}_{n}),\bm{M}_{j}^{\perp}\rangle\right]

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\eta\sum_{\bm{X}_{n,s}\in\mathfrak{N}}\mathbb{E}\!\left[\ell^{\prime}_{s,t}\,h_{i,t}(\bm{X}_{n,s})\langle\nabla_{\bm{w}_{i}}h(\bm{X}_{n}),\bm{M}_{j}^{\perp}\rangle\right]+\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-\eta\lambda)\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}^{\perp}\rangle\;\pm\;\widetilde{O}\!\left(\frac{\eta\sum_{i\in[m]}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}}{\tau d}\cdot\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}\right)\;\pm\;\widetilde{O}\!\left(\eta\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}\right).

Then we can begin to perform our induction: at
t=0
, our properties holds trivially. Now suppose before iteration
t=t_{1}
, the claimed properties holds, then we can easily obtain that for all
t\leq t_{1}
:

\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}=\|\bm{M}\bm{M}^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\bm{M}^{\perp}(\bm{M}^{\perp})^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}\overset{\text{(1)}}{\leq}O(1)\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\|_{2}^{2}\overset{\text{(2)}}{\leq}\frac{1}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}.

(257)

(1) is Using Lemma
C.1
along with the Lemma
B.2
.

\displaystyle\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\bm{M}\bm{M}^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\bm{M}^{\perp}(\bm{M}^{\perp})^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}

(258)

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\bm{M}\bm{M}^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\right\|_{2}^{2}\left(1+\epsilon_{\max}\tfrac{\eta C_{z}\log\log d}{d}\right)^{2t}+\left\|\bm{M}^{\perp}(\bm{M}^{\perp})^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\bm{M}\bm{M}^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\right\|_{2}^{2}\cdot\Theta(\frac{d_{1}}{d})+\left\|\bm{M}^{\perp}(\bm{M}^{\perp})^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{d}{d_{1}}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\|_{2}^{2}\cdot\Theta(\frac{d_{1}}{d})+\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left(1+\epsilon_{\max}C_{z}\log d\right)\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq O(1)\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\|_{2}^{2}.

(2) is because:

\displaystyle\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{0}^{2}d_{1}\left(1+\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{1}}}\right)\right)

(259)

\displaystyle\leq\Theta\left(\frac{1}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}\right)d_{1}\left(1+\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{1}}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}.

Thus we have
\sum_{i\in[m]}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{1}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}
. We now begin to verify all the properties for
t=t_{1}+1
, until
t_{1}
reaches
T_{1}
.

We first derive an upper bound for

\left\|\bm{M}\bm{M}^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\right\|_{2}^{2},

(260)

at iterations
t\leq t_{1}
. For each
j\in[d]
, as long as

\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\geq\Omega\left(\frac{\left\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\right\|_{2}}{d\sqrt{d_{1}}}\right),

(261)

then

\displaystyle|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|

\displaystyle=\left(1-\eta\lambda+\epsilon_{j}\frac{\eta C_{z}\log\log d}{d}\right)\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle

(262)

\displaystyle\pm\widetilde{O}\left(\eta\frac{\sum_{i\in[m]}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}}{\tau d}\cdot\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}\right)\pm\widetilde{O}\left(\eta\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\eta\lambda+\epsilon_{j}\frac{\eta C_{z}\log\log d}{d}\right)\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle+\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(1+\epsilon_{j}\frac{\eta C_{z}\log\log d}{d}\right)\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle+\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}\right)-\eta\lambda\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+\epsilon_{j}\frac{\eta C_{z}\log\log d}{d}\right)\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle+\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{d_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(1+\epsilon_{j}\frac{\eta C_{z}\log\log d}{d}\right)\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle+\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{d_{1}}\sqrt{d_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(1+\epsilon_{j}\frac{\eta C_{z}\log\log d}{d}\right)\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle+\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{d^{6}}\sqrt{d_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+\epsilon_{j}\frac{\eta C_{z}\log\log d}{d}\right)\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle+\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\eta}{d^{2}}\right)\Omega\left(\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{d\sqrt{d_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+\epsilon_{j}\frac{\eta C_{z}\log\log d}{d}\right)\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle+\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\eta}{d^{2}}\right)\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+\epsilon_{j}\frac{\eta C_{z}\log\log d}{d}\right)|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|+\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\eta}{d^{2}}\right)|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|

\displaystyle=\left(1+\epsilon_{j}\frac{\eta C_{z}\log\log d}{d}+\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\eta}{d^{2}}\right)\right)|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|.

Define set of features:

\mathcal{E}^{(t)}:=\left\{j\in[d]:\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle<\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\left\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\right\|_{2}}{d\sqrt{d_{1}}}\right)\right\},

(263)

note that
\mathcal{E}^{(t+1)}\subseteq\mathcal{E}^{(t)}\subseteq\Lambda_{i}

(where the set
\Lambda_{i}
is defined in Lemma
D.2
) in the sense that if
j\notin\mathcal{E}^{(t)}
, then

\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\geq\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\left\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\right\|_{2}}{d\sqrt{d_{1}}}\right)\Rightarrow\epsilon_{j}\frac{C_{z}\log\log d}{d}\left|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\right|\geq\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\left\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\right\|_{2}}{d_{1}}\right),

(264)

in the above calculations. Therefore:

H.3
Proof of Lemma
C.1
(a):

Proof of Lemma
C.1
(a):

\displaystyle\|\bm{M}\bm{M}^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in[d]}\left[\left(1-\eta\lambda+\epsilon_{j}\frac{\eta C_{z}\log\log d}{d}\right)\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\pm\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}\right)\right]^{2}

(265)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{\textcircled{\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{1}}}}}{\leq}\sum_{j\in[d]}\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle^{2}\left(1+\epsilon_{j}\frac{\eta C_{z}\log\log d}{d}+\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\eta}{d^{2}}\right)\right)^{2t+2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j\in[d]:j\in\mathcal{E}^{(0)}}\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{(t+1)^{2}\eta^{2}\max_{t\leq t_{1}}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{\textcircled{\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{2}}}}}{\leq}\|\bm{M}\bm{M}^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\|_{2}^{2}\left(1+\epsilon_{\max}\frac{\eta C_{z}\log\log d}{d}+\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\eta}{d^{2}}\right)\right)^{2t+2}

\displaystyle+O(1/d)\|\bm{M}\bm{M}^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\|_{2}^{2}.

\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{1}}⃝
is because:

\displaystyle\|\bm{M}\bm{M}^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}^{2}

(266)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in[d]}\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in[d]}\left[\left(1-\eta\lambda+\epsilon_{j}\frac{\eta C_{z}\log\log d}{d}\right)\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\pm\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}\right)\right]^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in[d]:j\notin\mathcal{E}^{(t)}}\left[\left(1-\eta\lambda+\epsilon_{j}\frac{\eta C_{z}\log\log d}{d}\right)\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\pm\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}\right)\right]^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in[d]:j\in\mathcal{E}^{(t)}}\left[\left(1-\eta\lambda+\epsilon_{j}\frac{\eta C_{z}\log\log d}{d}\right)\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\pm\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}\right)\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in[d]:j\notin\mathcal{E}^{(t)}}\left[\left(1+\epsilon_{j}\frac{\eta C_{z}\log\log d}{d}+\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\eta}{d^{2}}\right)\right)\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\right]^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in[d]:j\in\mathcal{E}^{(t)}}\left[\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle+\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}\right)\right]^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in[d]:j\notin\mathcal{E}^{(0)}}\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle^{2}\left(1+\epsilon_{j}\frac{\eta C_{z}\log\log d}{d}+\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\eta}{d^{2}}\right)\right)^{2(t+1)}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in[d]:j\in\mathcal{E}^{(0)}}\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{(t+1)^{2}\eta^{2}\max_{t\leq t_{1}}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}\right).

\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{2}}⃝
holds because for all
t\leq t_{1}\leq T_{1}=\Theta\!\left(\tfrac{d_{1}\log d}{\eta\log\log d}\right)
, the final inequality follows from the calculations below, together with the bound

\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}\leq O(1)\,\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\|_{2}^{2}
and Lemma
B.2
.

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in[d]:j\in\mathcal{E}^{(0)}}\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{(t+1)^{2}\eta^{2}\max_{t\leq t_{1}}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}\right)

(267)

\displaystyle\leq\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{d\cdot d_{1}^{2}\cdot\max_{t\leq t_{1}}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}\right)\ll\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\|_{2}^{2}}{d_{1}}=O(\frac{1}{d})\|\mathbf{M}\mathbf{M}^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\|_{2}^{2}.

The proof of the upper bound actually implies two information: (1)
\left\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\right\|_{2}/\left(d\sqrt{d_{1}}\right)
serves as a threshold. At time step
t
, if the component of
\bm{w}_{i}
along
\bm{M}_{j}
exceeds this threshold, then this
\bm{w}_{i}
along
\bm{M}_{j}
significantly contributes to learning over the entire space; otherwise, its contribution to the learning of the overall space is negligible. (2) The number of features in
\mathcal{E}^{(t)}
decreases over time.
∎

Proof of Lemma

(b):

Proof of Lemma
C.1
(b):

\displaystyle\|\bm{M}\bm{M}^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}^{2}

(268)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in[d]}\left[\left(1-\eta\lambda+\epsilon_{j}\frac{\eta C_{z}\log\log d}{d}\right)\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\pm\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}\right)\right]^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in[d]}\left[\left(1-\eta\lambda+\epsilon_{j}\frac{\eta C_{z}\log\log d}{d}\right)^{2}\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t-1)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle+2\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}\right)\right.

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\left.\left(1-\eta\lambda+\epsilon_{j}\frac{\eta C_{z}\log\log d}{d}\right)\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}\right)\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in[d]}\left[\left(1-\eta\lambda+\epsilon_{j}\frac{\eta C_{z}\log\log d}{d}\right)^{2}\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t-1)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle+2\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}\right)\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in[d]}\left[\left(1-\eta\lambda+\epsilon_{j}\frac{\eta C_{z}\log\log d}{d}\right)^{t+1}\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle+\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{(t+1)\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}\right)\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in\mathcal{E}^{(0)}}\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle^{2}\left(1-\eta\lambda+\epsilon_{j}\frac{\eta C_{z}\log\log d}{d}\right)^{2t+2}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{(t+1)^{2}\eta^{2}d\max_{t\leq t_{1}}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\|\bm{M}\bm{M}^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\|_{2}^{2}\left(1-\eta\lambda+\epsilon_{\min}\frac{\eta C_{z}\log\log d}{d}\right)^{2t+2}-O(1/d)\|\bm{M}\bm{M}^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\|_{2}^{2}.

We aim to obtain the maximum of the lower bound. The extreme case here is when all
j
belong to the set
\mathcal{E}^{(0)}
, meaning that the components of
\bm{w}_{i}
on all feature
\bm{M}_{j}
are smaller than the critical value
\left\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\right\|_{2}/\left(d\sqrt{d_{1}}\right)
. where the last inequality follows from our computations of the upper bound.
∎

Proof of Lemma

Finally we give an upper bound of

\left\|\bm{M}^{\perp}(\bm{M}^{\perp})^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\right\|_{2}^{2}

(269)

for iterations
t\leq t_{1}
. We can calculate similarly, by

\displaystyle\|\bm{M}^{\perp}(\bm{M}^{\perp})^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}^{2}

(270)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in[d_{1}]\setminus[d]}\left[(1-\eta\lambda)\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}^{\perp}\rangle\pm\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\text{ploy}(d_{1})}\right)\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in[d_{1}]\setminus[d]}\left[\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}^{\perp}\rangle+\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}\right)\right]^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in[d_{1}]\setminus[d]}\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)},\bm{M}_{j}^{\perp}\rangle^{2}+\sum_{j\in[d_{1}]\setminus[d]}2\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)},\bm{M}_{j}^{\perp}\rangle\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\eta(t+1)\max_{t\leq t_{1}}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}\right)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in[d_{1}]\setminus[d]}\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\eta(t+1)\max_{t\leq t_{1}}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\bm{M}^{\perp}(\bm{M}^{\perp})^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\|_{2}^{2}+\max_{j\in[d_{1}]\setminus[d]}|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)},\bm{M}_{j}^{\perp}\rangle|\widetilde{O}\left(\max_{t\leq t_{1}}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}\right)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\overset{\text{\textcircled{\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{1}}}}}{\leq}

\displaystyle(1+\widetilde{O}(d/\sqrt{d_{1}}))\|\bm{M}^{\perp}(\bm{M}^{\perp})^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\|_{2}^{2}+O(1/\operatorname{poly}(d_{1}))\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{\textcircled{\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{2}}}}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(1+\frac{1}{\operatorname{poly}(d)}\right)\|\bm{M}^{\perp}(\bm{M}^{\perp})^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\|_{2}^{2}.

\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{1}}⃝
is because: at initialization, we have
\left|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)},\bm{M}_{j}^{\perp}\rangle\right|\leq\widetilde{O}\left(\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\|/\sqrt{d_{1}}\right)
with high probability; from our Lemma
C.1

\widetilde{O}\left(\max_{t\leq t_{1}}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}\right)\leq O(1)\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\|_{2}^{2}
; at initialization we have
\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\|_{2}^{2}\leq O\left(\|\bm{M}^{\perp}(\bm{M}^{\perp})^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\|_{2}^{2}\right)
with high probability and
t=\Theta\left(\frac{d_{1}\log d}{\eta\log\log d}\right)

\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{2}}⃝
is because: at initialization we have
\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\|_{2}^{2}\leq O\left(\|\bm{M}^{\perp}(\bm{M}^{\perp})^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\|_{2}^{2}\right)
with high probability

Note that for each neuron
i\in[m]
, from Lemma
B.2
combined with our upper bound and lower bound, we know when all the weights
\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}
reach
\Theta(1)\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)}\|_{2}^{2}
, the maximum:

\max_{i\in[m]}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}^{2}\leq O(1)(\left\|\bm{M}\bm{M}^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left\|\bm{M}^{\perp}(\bm{M}^{\perp})^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\right\|_{2}^{2}=O(1)\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}^{2})

(271)

for all
t\leq t_{1}
. Thus we have obtained all the results for
t=t_{1}+1
, and are able to proceed induction.
∎

H.6
Proof of Lemma
C.2
:

Proof of Lemma
C.2
:

\displaystyle\Pr\left[\left|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\right|\geq\Omega(\sigma_{0}\log^{1/4}d)\right]\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{t^{2}}{2\sigma_{0}^{2}}\right)=2^{-\Omega(\sqrt{\log d})}.

(272)

Fix
i\in[m]
, and consider
d
different
j
. Each has probability
2^{-\Omega(\sqrt{\log d})}
to exceed the threshold. Therefore, the expectation is:

\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\left\{j\in[d]\;\middle|\;\left|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\right|\geq\Omega\left(\sigma_{0}\log^{1/4}d\right)\right\}\right|\right]=O\left(2^{-\sqrt{\log d}}\cdot d\right).

(273)

∎

Proof of Lemmas in Appendix D

I.1
Proof of Lemma

(a):

Proof of Lemma
D.1
(a):

At iteration
t=T_{1}
, we have verified all the above properties in Theorem C.1. Now suppose all the properties hold for
t<T_{2}
, we will verify that it still hold for
t+1
. In order to calculate the gradient
\nabla_{\bm{w}_{i}}L_{\mathrm{aug}}
along each feature
\bm{M}_{j}
or
\bm{M}_{j}^{\perp}
, we have to apply Lemma
E.10
, Lemma
E.11
and Lemma
F.3
. First we calculate parameters in Lemma
E.10
(a) and (b). In order to using Lemma
E.10
, we have the followings

\left|\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}_{i,t}\right|^{2}=\left|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\frac{z_{Y}^{(s)\setminus j}-z_{X}^{(r)\setminus j}}{2}\rangle\right|^{2}\leq\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}}{d}\right)

(274)

\Pr(A_{1}),\Pr(A_{2})\leq e^{-\Omega(\log^{1/4}d)}\quad\text{when }|\alpha^{(t)}_{i,j}|\leq\sqrt{(1-\gamma)}b_{i}^{(t)}

(275)

\Pr(A_{3}),\Pr(A_{4})\leq e^{-\Omega(\log^{1/4}d)}\quad\text{when }|\alpha^{(t)}_{i,j}|\geq\sqrt{(1+\gamma)}b_{i}^{(t)}

(276)

Which further implies that when
(|\alpha_{i,j}^{(t)}|\leq\sqrt{(1-\gamma)})

\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[|\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}|^{2}(\bm{1}_{A_{1}}+\bm{1}_{A_{2}})]}\leq\sqrt{\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}}{d}\right)\left(\Pr(A_{1})+\Pr(A_{2})\right)}\overset{(1)}{\leq}\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{d}\cdot\operatorname{polylog}(d)}

(277)

(1) is because:
e^{-\Omega(\log^{1/4}d)}\leq\frac{1}{\operatorname{polylog}(d)}
,

L_{1}
is because:

L_{1}:=\sqrt{\frac{\mathbb{E}[|\bar{S}_{i,t}^{(r,s)\setminus j}|^{2}(\bm{1}_{A_{1}}+\bm{1}_{A_{2}})]}{\mathbb{E}[\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\frac{\tilde{\xi}_{n}+\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{+}}{2}\rangle^{2}]}}\leq\sqrt{\frac{\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}}{d\cdot\operatorname{polylog}(d)}}{\Theta\left(\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}\sqrt{\log d}}{d}\right)}}\leq\frac{1}{\operatorname{polylog}(d)}

(278)

L_{2}=\Pr(A_{2})
holds as well

\Rightarrow L_{1},L_{2}\leq\frac{1}{\operatorname{polylog}(d)}

(279)

And similarly, we also have
L_{3},L_{4}\leq\frac{1}{\operatorname{polylog}(d)}
when
|\alpha_{i,j}^{(t)}|\geq\sqrt{(1+\gamma)}b_{i}^{(t)}
.

Now we separately discuss three cases:

(a) When
i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}^{\star}
, if
\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}
and
\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\neq 0
, say
\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}}{2}=C^{(r,s)}_{\tilde{z}}
, we simply have

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\left|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle|\geq b_{i}+|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}-\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle\right|\right)

(280)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 1-\Pr\left(\left|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\frac{z_{X}^{(r)\setminus j}+z_{Y}^{(s)\setminus j}}{2}\rangle\right|\geq b_{i}^{(t)}-\alpha_{i,j}^{(t)}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}}{2}+\left|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}-\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle\right|\right)

From the observations that:
(1)
\left|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\frac{z_{X}^{(r)\setminus j}+z_{Y}^{(s)\setminus j}}{2}\rangle\right|\leq\gamma b_{i}^{(t)}
with probability
\geq 1-e^{-\Omega(\log^{1/4}d)}
; (2)
\left|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}-\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle\right|\leq O(\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}\sigma_{\xi})
with prob
\geq 1-e^{-\Omega(\log^{1/2}d)}
.

So it can be easily verified that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle|\geq b_{i}+|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}-\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle|}\right]

(281)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]\Pr\left(|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\frac{z^{(r)}_{X}+z^{(s)}_{Y}}{2}\rangle|\geq b_{i}+|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}-\frac{z^{(r)}_{X}+z^{(s)}_{Y}}{2}|\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\epsilon_{j}\frac{L^{2}C_{z}\log\log d}{d}\left(1-\frac{1}{\operatorname{polylog}(d)}\right)

Now we can compute as follows: for
\bm{M}_{j}
such that
i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}^{\star}
, at iteration
t+1
:

\displaystyle\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle

(282)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle-\eta\langle\nabla_{\bm{w}_{i}}L_{\mathrm{aug}}(f_{t}),\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\pm\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle(1-\eta\lambda)\pm\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\eta\mathbb{E}\left[(1-\ell_{p,t}^{t})h_{i,t}(\bm{Y}_{n})\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\langle\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\right]

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\eta\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{X\in\mathfrak{N}}\ell_{s,t}^{t}h_{i,t}(X)\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\langle\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\text{\textcircled{\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{1}}}}}{=}

\displaystyle\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle(1-\eta\lambda)\pm\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\eta\frac{1}{L}\mathbb{E}\left[(1-\ell_{p,t}^{t})h_{i,t}(\bm{Y}_{n})\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}(\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\langle\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle)\right]

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\eta\frac{1}{L}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{X\in\mathfrak{N}}\ell_{s,t}^{t}h_{i,t}(X)\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}(\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\langle\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle)\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\text{\textcircled{\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{2}}}}}{\geq}

\displaystyle\left(\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle-\operatorname{sign}(\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle)\cdot b_{i}^{(t)}\right)\cdot\left(1-\eta\lambda+\epsilon_{j}\frac{\eta C_{z}\log\log d}{d}\left(1-\frac{1}{\operatorname{polylog}(d)}\right)\right)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle O\left(\frac{\eta|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|}{d\cdot\operatorname{polylog}(d)}\right)\pm O\left(\frac{\eta\sum_{i^{\prime}\in[m]}\|w_{i^{\prime}}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{d\tau}\right)\pm\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{d\sqrt{d_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{\textcircled{\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{3}}}}}{\geq}

\displaystyle\left(\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle-\operatorname{sign}(\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle)\cdot b_{i}^{(t)}\right)\cdot\left(1+\epsilon_{j}\frac{\eta C_{z}\log\log d}{d}\left(1-\frac{\eta}{\operatorname{polylog}(d)}\right)\right)

\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{1}}⃝
is because
\langle\bm{M}\tilde{z}_{n}^{(r)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle=\left\langle\sum_{k=1}^{d}\tilde{z}_{n,k}^{(r)}\bm{M}_{k},\bm{M}_{j}\right\rangle=\sum_{k=1}^{d}\tilde{z}_{n,k}^{(r)}\langle\bm{M}_{k},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle=\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}
.
\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{2}}⃝
is because Lemma
E.11
.
\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{3}}⃝
is because we have taken into consideration

\sum_{i\in[m]}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\sum_{i\in[m]}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(T_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\sum_{i\in[m]}d\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(T_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{1}{\operatorname{poly}(d)}

(283)

which follows from our definition of iteration
T_{2}
and the properties at iteration
T_{1}
in Theorem C.1, and also that

\frac{\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle}{d}\geq\frac{b_{i}^{(t)}}{d}\geq\frac{b_{i}^{(T_{1})}}{d}\geq\sqrt{\frac{2\log d}{d^{3}}}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(T_{1})}\|_{2}\geq\sqrt{\frac{2\log d}{d^{4}}}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(T_{2})}\|_{2}\gg\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{d_{1}}}

(284)

Next we compare this growth to the growth of bias
b_{i}^{(t+1)}
. Since we raise our bias by

b_{i}^{(t+1)}=\max\left\{b_{i}^{(t)}(1+\frac{\eta}{d}),b_{i}^{(t)}\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}}{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}\right\},

(285)

The above inequality
\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\geq\left(\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle-\operatorname{sign}(\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle)\cdot b_{i}^{(t)}\right)\cdot\left(1+\frac{\eta}{d}\right)
has already verified the case of
(1+\frac{\eta}{d})
. as long as

\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}}{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}\leq\frac{|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|}{|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|},

(286)

we can obtain the desired result

\left(\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}}{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}\leq\frac{|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|}{|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|}\right)
. This will be proved later, after we prove (d).
∎

I.2
Proof of Lemma
D.1
(b):

Proof of Lemma
D.1
(b):

When
i\notin\mathcal{M}_{j}
, we can similarly obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle|\geq b_{i}+|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}-\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle|}\right]

(287)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\epsilon_{j}\frac{L^{2}C_{z}\log\log d}{d}\left(\frac{1}{\operatorname{polylog}(d)}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\left(\epsilon_{j}\frac{L^{2}}{d\cdot\operatorname{polylog}(d)}\right)

And similarly we can compute the gradient descent dynamics as follows:

For
j\in[d]
such that
|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|\geq\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}d}{\sqrt{d_{1}}}
, we have
(assume here
\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle>0
, the opposite is similar)

\displaystyle\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle

(288)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle-\eta\langle\nabla_{\bm{w}_{i}}L_{\mathrm{aug}}(f_{t}),\bm{M}_{j}\rangle+\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\left(1-\eta\lambda+\epsilon_{j}\frac{O(\eta)}{d\cdot\operatorname{polylog}(d)}\right)\pm O\left(\frac{\eta\sum_{i^{\prime}\in[m]}\|w_{i^{\prime}}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{d\tau}\right)\pm\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{d^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\left(1+\epsilon_{j}\frac{O(\eta)}{d\cdot\operatorname{polylog}(d)}\right)+\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{d^{2}}\right)

Since from our update rule

b_{i}^{(t+1)}\geq b_{i}^{(t)}\left(1+\frac{\eta}{d}\right)

(289)

we know that

\displaystyle b_{i}^{(t+1)}\geq\left(1+\frac{\eta}{d}\right)b_{i}^{(t)}\geq\left(1+\frac{O(\eta)}{d\cdot\operatorname{polylog}(d)}\right)b_{i}^{(t)}\geq\frac{|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|}{|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|}b_{i}^{(t)}

(290)

Thus, if
|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|\leq\sqrt{1-\gamma}b_{i}^{(t)}
at iteration
t
, we have

|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|\leq\frac{b_{i}^{(t+1)}|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|}{b_{i}^{(t)}}\leq\sqrt{1-\gamma}b_{i}^{(t+1)}\quad\text{if }|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|\geq\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}d}{\sqrt{d_{1}}}\text{ at iteration }t;

|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|\leq\sqrt{1-\gamma}b_{i}^{(t+1)}\quad\text{if }|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|\leq\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}d}{\sqrt{d_{1}}}\text{ at iteration }t.

It is also worth noting that similar calculations also lead to a lower bound:

|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|\geq|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|(1-\eta\lambda)-\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\eta\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{d^{2}}\right)

(291)

We leave the part of proving
|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|\leq\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{d}}\right)
to later.
∎

I.3
Proof of Lemma
D.1
(d):

Proof of Lemma
D.1
(d):

Next we consider the learning dynamics for the dense features. We can use Lemma
E.11
to calculate its dynamics by

\displaystyle\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)},\bm{M}_{j}^{\perp}\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}^{\perp}\rangle(1-\eta\lambda)\pm\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}

(292)

\displaystyle+\eta\mathbb{E}\left[(1-\ell_{p,t}^{\prime})h_{i,t}(\bm{Y}_{n})\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\langle\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)},\bm{M}_{j}^{\perp}\rangle\right]

\displaystyle-\eta\sum_{X\in\mathfrak{N}}\mathbb{E}\left[\ell_{s,t}^{\prime}h_{i,t}(X)\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\langle\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)},\bm{M}_{j}^{\perp}\rangle\right]

\displaystyle=\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}^{\perp}\rangle(1-\eta\lambda)\pm\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\operatorname{poly}(d_{1})}

\displaystyle+\eta\mathbb{E}\left[(1-\ell_{p,t}^{\prime})h_{i,t}(\bm{Y}_{n})\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\langle\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)},\bm{M}_{j}^{\perp}\rangle\right]

\displaystyle-\eta\sum_{X\in\mathfrak{N}}\mathbb{E}\left[\ell_{s,t}^{\prime}h_{i,t}(X)\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\langle\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)},\bm{M}_{j}^{\perp}\rangle\right]

\displaystyle=\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}^{\perp}\rangle(1-\eta\lambda)+\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{d\sqrt{d_{1}}}\right)\cdot\Pr\left(h_{i,t}(\bm{Y}_{n})\neq 0\right)

\displaystyle\leq\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}^{\perp}\rangle+O\left(\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{d\sqrt{d_{1}}}e^{-\Omega(\log^{1/4}d)}\right)

∎

I.4
Supplement to Lemma
D.1
(a):

After establishing the bounds of growth speed for each feature, we now calculate the proportions they contribute to each neuron weight
i\in[m]
. Namely, we need to prove that when Lemma
D.1
holds at iteration
t\in[T_{1},T_{2}]
, we have to prove:

\frac{|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|}{|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|}\geq\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}}{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}\quad\text{for }i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}^{\star},

(293)

we argue as follows: from previous calculations we have:

\displaystyle\sum_{j^{\prime}\in[d],j^{\prime}\neq j}\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)},\bm{M}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle^{2}+\sum_{j^{\prime}\in[d_{1}]\setminus[d]}\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)},\bm{M}_{j^{\prime}}^{\perp}\rangle^{2}

(294)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j^{\prime}\in[d],j^{\prime}\neq j}\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle^{2}\left(1+\epsilon_{j^{\prime}}\frac{O(\eta)}{d\operatorname{polylog}(d)}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j^{\prime}\in[d_{1}]\setminus[d]}\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j^{\prime}}^{\perp}\rangle^{2}+\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\eta}{d}\right)e^{-\Omega(\log^{1/4}d)}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}

Therefore by adding
\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle^{2}
to the LHS we have:

\displaystyle\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}\left(1+\epsilon_{\max}\frac{O(\eta)}{d\cdot\operatorname{polylog}(d)}\right)^{2}

(295)

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|}{|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|}-\frac{O(\eta)}{d\cdot\operatorname{polylog}(d)}\right)|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|^{2}

which implies

\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}^{2}}{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}}\leq\left(1+\epsilon_{\max}\frac{O(\eta)}{d\cdot\operatorname{polylog}(d)}\right)^{2}+\left(\frac{|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|}{|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|}\right)\frac{|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|^{2}}{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}}\

(296)

Therefore,
\frac{|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|}{|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|}\geq\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}}{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}
is as desired.

I.5
Supplement to Lemma
D.1
(b):

To prove
|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|\leq\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{d}}\right)
if
i\notin\mathcal{M}_{j}
, we first use inequality to compute

\displaystyle\|\bm{MM}^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}

(297)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\|\bm{MM}^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(T_{1})}\|_{2}(1-\eta\lambda)^{t-T_{1}+1}-O\left(\frac{\eta(t-T_{1}+1)\max_{t^{\prime}\in[T_{1},t+1]}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t^{\prime})}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{dd_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\|\bm{MM}^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(T_{1})}\|_{2}(1-\eta\lambda)^{t-T_{1}+1}-O\left(\frac{\eta(t-T_{1})\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(T_{1})}\|_{2}\sqrt{d}}{\sqrt{d_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\|\bm{MM}^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(T_{1})}\|_{2}(1-o(1))\quad\text{for}\quad t\leq\Theta\left(\frac{d\log d}{\eta\log\log d}\right)

Notice that

|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(T_{1})},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|\leq\sqrt{1-\gamma}\frac{\sqrt{2\log d}}{\sqrt{d}}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(T_{1})}\|_{2}\leq O\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log d}{d}}\right)\left\|\bm{MM}^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(T_{1})}\right\|_{2}

(298)

From Theorem
C.1
. Suppose it also holds for iteration t, we have

\displaystyle|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|

(299)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|\left(1+\epsilon_{j}\frac{O(\eta)}{d\,\operatorname{polylog}(d)}\right)+\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{d^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(T_{1})},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|\left(1+\epsilon_{j}\frac{O(\eta)}{d\,\operatorname{polylog}(d)}\right)^{t-T_{1}}+\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\eta(t-T_{1})\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(T_{1})}\|_{2}}{d^{3/2}}\right)

\displaystyle\text{(because}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{d}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(T_{1})}\|_{2}\text{ by the definition of }T_{2})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(T_{1})},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|(1+o(1)\frac{\epsilon_{j}}{\epsilon_{\max}})+\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(T_{1})}\|_{2}}{d}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{\textcircled{\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{1}}}}}{\leq}

\displaystyle O\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log d}{d}}\right)\left\|\bm{MM}^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(T_{1})}\right\|_{2}(1+o(1)\frac{\epsilon_{j}}{\epsilon_{\max}})

\displaystyle\overset{\text{\textcircled{\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{2}}}}}{\leq}

\displaystyle O\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log d}{d}}\right)\left\|\bm{MM}^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\right\|_{2}(1+o(1)\frac{\epsilon_{j}}{\epsilon_{\max}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log d}{d}}\right)\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}(1+o(1)\frac{\epsilon_{j}}{\epsilon_{\max}})

\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{1}}⃝
and
\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{2}}⃝
use the above inequality respectively.

I.6
Supplement to Lemma
D.1
(d):

For the dense features, we can compute as follows:

\displaystyle|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)},\bm{M}_{j}^{\perp}\rangle|

(300)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}^{\perp}\rangle|+O\left(\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{d\sqrt{d_{1}}}e^{-\Omega(\log^{1/4}d)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(T_{1})},\bm{M}_{j}^{\perp}\rangle|+\sum_{t^{\prime}=T_{1}}^{t}O\left(\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t^{\prime})}\|_{2}}{d\sqrt{d_{1}}}e^{-\Omega(\log^{1/4}d)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log d}{d_{1}}}\right)\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(T_{1}+1)}\|_{2}\left(1+\frac{O(\eta)}{d\,\mathrm{polylog}(d)}\right)^{2}+\sum_{t^{\prime}=T_{1}+1}^{t}O\left(\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t^{\prime})}\|_{2}}{d\sqrt{d_{1}}}e^{-\Omega(\log^{1/4}d)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log d}{d_{1}}}\right)\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}\left(1+\frac{O(\eta)}{d\,\mathrm{polylog}(d)}\right)^{2(t-T_{1}+1)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log d}{d_{1}}}\right)\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}

where we have used the assumption that

\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}\leq\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}\left(1+\frac{O(\eta)}{d\,\operatorname{polylog}(d)}\right)
for all
i\in[m]
and all
t\leq T_{2}=T_{1}+\Theta\left(\frac{d\tau\log d}{\epsilon_{\max}\eta\log\log d}\right)
.

Which we prove here: First of all, from previous calculations we have

\left\|\bm{MM}^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\right\|_{2}\geq\left\|\bm{MM}^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\right\|_{2}(1-\eta\lambda)-O\left(\frac{\eta\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{d^{3/2}}e^{-\Omega(\log^{1/4}d)}\right)

(301)

also the trajectory of
\left\|\bm{M}^{\perp}(\bm{M}^{\perp})^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\right\|_{2}
can be lower bounded as

\left\|\bm{M}^{\perp}(\bm{M}^{\perp})^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\right\|_{2}\geq\left\|\bm{M}^{\perp}(\bm{M}^{\perp})^{\top}\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\right\|_{2}(1-\eta\lambda)-O\left(\frac{\eta}{d}\right)e^{-\Omega(\log^{1/4}d)}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}

(302)

thus by combining the change of
\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}
over two subspaces, we have

\displaystyle\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}(1-\eta\lambda)^{2}-O\left(\frac{\eta}{d}\right)e^{-\Omega(\log^{1/4}d)}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}

(303)

\displaystyle\geq\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}\left(1-\frac{O(\eta)}{d\,\operatorname{polylog}(d)}\right)

From the inequality above:

\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}^{2}\geq\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}(1-\varepsilon)\quad\text{where}\quad\varepsilon=\frac{O(\eta)}{d\cdot\operatorname{polylog}(d)}

(304)

Divide both sides by
1-\varepsilon
, we get:

\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}^{2}\cdot\frac{1}{1-\varepsilon}

(305)

Note that when
\varepsilon\ll 1
, we have:

\frac{1}{1-\varepsilon}=1+\varepsilon+\varepsilon^{2}+\cdots\leq 1+2\varepsilon\quad\text{(since }\varepsilon\text{ is very small)}

(306)

Therefore, we can write:

\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}^{2}(1+O(\varepsilon))

(307)

\displaystyle\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}\sqrt{(1+O(\varepsilon))}\leq\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}(1+O(\varepsilon))

(308)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t+1)}\|_{2}\left(1+\frac{O(\eta)}{d\cdot\operatorname{polylog}(d)}\right)

which gives the desired bound.

I.7
Supplement to Lemma
D.1
(
T_{2}
):

In the proof above, we have depended on the crucial assumption that
T_{2}:=\min\left\{t\in\mathbb{N}:\exists i\in[m]\text{ s.t. }\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}\geq d\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(T_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\right\}
is of order
T_{1}+\Theta\left(\frac{d\tau\log d}{\epsilon_{\max}\eta\log\log d}\right)
. Now we verify it as follows. If
i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}^{\star}
for some
j\in[d]
(which also means
j^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{N}_{i}\text{ for }j^{\prime}\neq j)
, we have

\displaystyle|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|

\displaystyle\geq|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(T_{1})},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|\left(1+\Omega\left(\epsilon_{j}\frac{\eta\log\log d}{d}\right)\right)^{t-T_{1}}

(309)

\displaystyle\geq d\sqrt{\frac{2\log d}{d}}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(T_{1})}\|_{2}

For some
t=T_{1}+O\left(\frac{d\tau\log d}{\epsilon_{\max}\eta\log\log d}\right)
and
\epsilon_{j}=\epsilon_{\max}

Thus for some
t=T_{1}+O\left(\frac{d\tau\log d}{\epsilon_{\max}\eta\log\log d}\right)
, we have
|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|^{2}\geq d\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(T_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}
, which proves that
T_{2}\leq T_{1}+O\left(\frac{d\tau\log d}{\epsilon_{\max}\eta\log\log d}\right)
.

These results are to verify that
\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}\geq d\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(T_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}
holds under the order of
T_{2}=T_{1}+\Theta\left(\frac{d\tau\log d}{\epsilon_{\max}\eta\log\log d}\right)
.

\displaystyle\left(1+\Omega\left(\epsilon_{j}\frac{\eta\log\log d}{d}\right)\right)^{\Theta\left(\frac{d\log d}{\epsilon_{\max}\eta\log\log d}\right)}

\displaystyle=\exp\left(\Omega\left(\epsilon_{j}\frac{\eta\log\log d}{d}\right)\cdot\Theta\left(\frac{d\log d}{\epsilon_{\max}\eta\log\log d}\right)\right)

(310)

\displaystyle=\exp(\Omega(\frac{\epsilon_{j}}{\epsilon_{\max}}\log d))

\displaystyle=d^{\Omega(\frac{\epsilon_{j}}{\epsilon_{\max}})}

Conversely, we also have for all
t\leq T_{1}+O\left(\frac{d\tau\log d}{\epsilon_{\max}\eta\log\log d}\right)

\displaystyle\sum_{j^{\prime}\in[d]:j^{\prime}\neq j}\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle^{2}+\sum_{j^{\prime}\in[d_{1}]\setminus[d]}\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}^{\perp}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle^{2}

(311)

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(T_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\left(1+\epsilon_{j^{\prime}}\frac{O(\eta)}{d\,\operatorname{polylog}(d)}\right)^{t-T_{1}}+\max_{t^{\prime}\leq t}O\left(\frac{\eta(t-T_{1})}{d}\right)e^{-\Omega(\log^{1/4}d)}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t^{\prime})}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq o\left(d\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(T_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\right)

Except for the principal direction
\bm{M}_{j}
(i.e., the alignment direction of neuron i ), the total growth of squared weights along all other directions remains far below the target scale
d\cdot\left\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(T_{1})}\right\|_{2}^{2}.

And also

\displaystyle|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|\leq|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(T_{1})},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|\left(1+\epsilon_{j}\frac{C_{z}\eta\log\log d}{d}\left(1-\frac{1}{\operatorname{polylog}(d)}\right)\right)^{t-T_{1}}

(312)

\displaystyle\leq O\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log d}{d}}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(T_{1})}\|_{2}\right)\left(1+\epsilon_{j}\frac{C_{z}\eta\log\log d}{d}\left(1-\frac{1}{\operatorname{polylog}(d)}\right)\right)^{t-T_{1}}

Therefore we at least need
T_{1}+\frac{d\log\left(\Omega\left(\sqrt{d}\sqrt{\frac{d}{\log d}}\right)\right)}{\epsilon_{\max}\eta C_{z}\log\log d}(1-o(1))
iteration to let any neuron
i\in[m]
reach
\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}\geq d\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(T_{1})}\|_{2}
, which proves that
T_{2}=T_{1}+\Theta\left(\frac{d\tau\log d}{\epsilon_{\max}\eta\log\log d}\right).

Definition I.1

(Notations)
.

For simpler presentation, we define the following notations: given
\bm{z_{X}}=\frac{1}{L}(\bm{M}\tilde{z}_{n}+\tilde{\xi}_{n})\sim\mathcal{D}_{\bm{z_{X}}}
,

\bm{z_{Y}}=\frac{1}{L}(\bm{M}\tilde{z}_{n}^{+}+\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{+})\sim\mathcal{D}_{\bm{z_{Y}}}
, we let (for each
j\in[d]
):

z_{X}^{\setminus j}:=\frac{1}{L}\!\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j^{\prime}\neq j\\
j^{\prime}\in[d]\end{subarray}}\bm{M}_{j^{\prime}}\tilde{z}_{n,j^{\prime}}+\tilde{\xi}_{n}\right),\quad z_{Y}^{\setminus j}:=\frac{1}{L}\!\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j^{\prime}\neq j\\
j^{\prime}\in[d]\end{subarray}}\bm{M}_{j^{\prime}}\tilde{z}_{n,j^{\prime}}^{+}+\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{+}\right)

(313)

S_{i,t}^{(r)\setminus j}:=\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},z_{X}^{(r)\setminus j}\rangle,\quad S_{i,t}^{(s)\setminus j}:=\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},z_{Y}^{(s)\setminus j}\rangle

(314)

S_{i,t}^{(r,s)\setminus j}:=\tfrac{1}{2}\!\left(S_{i,t}^{(r)\setminus j}+S_{i,t}^{(s)\setminus j}\right),\quad\bar{S}_{i,t}^{(r,s)\setminus j}:=\tfrac{1}{2}\!\left(S_{i,t}^{(s)\setminus j}-S_{i,t}^{(r)\setminus j}\right)

(315)

\alpha_{i,j}^{(t)}:=\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle,\quad\bar{\alpha}_{i,j}^{(r,s)(t)}:=\Big\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}-\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j}}{\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}\bm{M}_{j}\Big\rangle

(316)

Whenever the neuron index
i\in[m]
is clear from the context, we drop the subscript
i
and the time index
t
for notational simplicity.

I.8
Proof of Lemma
D.2

Proof of Lemma
D.2
:

We have
\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma_{0}^{2})
and we want to control is the event:

\left|\left\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)},\bm{M}_{j}\right\rangle\right|\leq\frac{\sigma_{0}}{d}.

(317)

To get Lemma
D.2
, we can use the standard Gaussian anti-concentration property near the mean.
∎

Appendix J
Proof of Lemmas in Appendix E

J.1
Useful Lemmas

Lemma J.1
.

For any
j^{\prime}\neq j
, we have

|\mathcal{M}_{j^{\prime}}\cap\mathcal{M}_{j}|\leq O(\log d),

(318)

with probability at least
1-o(1/d^{4})
.

J.2
Proof of Lemma
E.2
:

Proof of Lemma
E.2
:

We first decompose

\Psi_{i,j}^{(t)}=\Psi_{i,j,1}^{(t)}+\Psi_{i,j,2}^{(t)}

(319)

where

\Psi_{i,j,1}^{(t)}=\mathbb{E}\left[(\ell_{p,t}^{\prime}-1)\cdot\psi_{i,j}^{(t)}(\bm{Y}_{n})\cdot\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

(320)

\Psi_{i,j,2}^{(t)}=\sum_{\bm{X}_{n,s}\in\mathfrak{N}}\mathbb{E}\left[\ell_{s,t}^{\prime}\cdot\psi_{i,j}^{(t)}(X)\cdot\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

(321)

We first deal with
\Psi_{i,j,2}^{(t)}
. Using the notation
\bm{X}_{n}^{\setminus j}
: For each
z_{X}^{(r)\setminus j}:=\frac{1}{L}\left(\sum_{j^{\prime}\neq j}\bm{M}_{j^{\prime}}\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j^{\prime}}+\xi^{(r)}_{n}\right)
,

we can rewrite as:

\displaystyle\Psi_{i,j,2}^{(t)}

(322)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\bm{X}_{n,s}\in\mathfrak{N}}\mathbb{E}\left[\ell_{s,t}^{\prime}(\bm{X}_{n},\mathfrak{B})\cdot\psi_{i,j}^{(t)}(\bm{X}_{n,s})\cdot\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\bm{X}_{n,s}\in\mathfrak{N}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\ell_{s,t}^{\prime}(\bm{X}_{n},\mathfrak{B})-\ell_{s,t}^{\prime}(\bm{X}_{n}^{\setminus j},\mathfrak{B})\right)\cdot\psi_{i,j}^{(t)}(\bm{X}_{n,s})\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{\bm{X}_{n,s}\in\mathfrak{N}}\mathbb{E}\left[\ell_{s,t}^{\prime}(\bm{X}_{n}^{\setminus j},\mathfrak{B})\cdot\psi_{i,j}^{(t)}(\bm{X}_{n,s})\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle R_{1}+R_{2}

We now deal with the term
R_{1}
. Denoting
\widehat{X}_{n}^{(v)}
: For each
\widehat{z}^{(r)}_{X}(v)=\widetilde{z}^{(r)}_{X}+\frac{v}{L}\bm{M}_{j}\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j}
, for
v\in[0,1]
, by Newton-Leibniz formula and the basic fact that

\frac{d}{dr}\frac{e^{r}}{e^{r}+\sum_{s\neq r}e^{s}}=\frac{e^{r}}{e^{r}+\sum_{s\neq r}e^{s}}\left(1-\frac{e^{r}}{e^{r}+\sum_{s\neq r}e^{s}}\right),

(323)

we can rewrite
\ell_{s,t}^{\prime}(\widehat{X}_{n}(v),\mathfrak{B})
and
\ell_{p,t}^{\prime}(\widehat{X}_{n}(v),\mathfrak{B})
as

\widehat{\ell}^{\prime}_{s,t}(\nu):=\frac{e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}^{\setminus j})+\nu(f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n})-f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}^{\setminus j})),f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n,s})\rangle/\tau}}{\sum_{x\in\mathfrak{B}}e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}^{\setminus j})+\nu(f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n})-f_{t}(X_{p}^{\setminus j})),f_{t}(x)\rangle/\tau}}\equiv\ell_{s,t}^{\prime}(\widehat{X}_{n}(v),\mathfrak{B})

(324)

\displaystyle\widehat{\ell}^{\prime}_{p,t}(\nu):=\frac{e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}^{\setminus j})+\nu(f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n})-f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}^{\setminus j})),f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n})\rangle/\tau}}{\sum_{x\in\mathfrak{B}}e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}^{\setminus j})+\nu(f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n})-f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}^{\setminus j})),f_{t}(x)\rangle/\tau}}\equiv\ell_{p,t}^{\prime}(\widehat{X}_{n}(v),\mathfrak{B})

(325)

and we can then proceed to calculate as follows:

\displaystyle R_{1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\bm{X}_{n,s}\in\mathfrak{N}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\ell_{s,t}^{\prime}(\bm{X}_{n},\mathfrak{B})-\ell_{s,t}^{\prime}(\bm{X}_{n}^{\setminus j},\mathfrak{B})\right)\cdot\psi_{i,j}^{(t)}(\bm{X}_{n,s})\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

(326)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\bm{X}_{n,s}\in\mathfrak{N}}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{\tau}\left(\int_{0}^{1}\widehat{\ell}^{\prime}_{s,t}(\nu)(1-\widehat{\ell}^{\prime}_{s,t}(\nu))\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n})-f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}^{\setminus j}),f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n,s})\rangle d\nu\right.\right.

\displaystyle-\sum_{X_{n,u}\in\mathfrak{N}\setminus\{\bm{X}_{n,s}\}}\int_{0}^{1}\widehat{\ell}^{\prime}_{s,t}(\nu)\widehat{\ell}^{\prime}_{u,t}(\nu)\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n})-f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}^{\setminus j}),f_{t}(X^{\prime})\rangle d\nu

\displaystyle\left.\left.-\int_{0}^{1}\widehat{\ell}^{\prime}_{s,t}(\nu)\widehat{\ell}^{\prime}_{p,t}(\nu)\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n})-f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}^{\setminus j}),f_{t}(\bm{Y}_{n})\rangle d\nu\right)\psi_{i,j}^{(t)}(\bm{X}_{n,s})\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\text{\textcircled{\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{1}}}}}{\leq}\frac{|\mathfrak{N}|}{\tau}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{1}\widehat{\ell}^{\prime}_{s,t}(\nu)(1-\widehat{\ell}^{\prime}_{s,t}(\nu))d\nu+\sum_{X_{n,u}\in\mathfrak{N}\setminus\{\bm{X}_{n,s}\}}\int_{0}^{1}\widehat{\ell}^{\prime}_{s,t}(\nu)\widehat{\ell}^{\prime}_{u,t}(\nu)d\nu\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\times\max_{X_{n,u}\in\mathfrak{N}\setminus\{\bm{X}_{n,s}\}}|\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n})-f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}^{\setminus j}),f_{t}(X_{n,u})\rangle|\cdot|\psi_{i,j}^{(t)}(\bm{X}_{n,s})|\cdot\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}|\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}|\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{|\mathfrak{N}|}{\tau}\int_{0}^{1}\widehat{\ell}^{\prime}_{s,t}(\nu)\widehat{\ell}^{\prime}_{p,t}(\nu)d\nu\cdot|\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n})-f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}^{\setminus j}),f_{t}(\bm{Y}_{n})\rangle|\cdot|\psi_{i,j}^{(t)}(\bm{X}_{n,s})|\cdot\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}|\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}|\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\text{\textcircled{\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{2}}}}}{\leq}\frac{|\mathfrak{B}|}{\tau}\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{0}^{1}\widehat{\ell}^{\prime}_{s,t}(\nu)d\nu\cdot\max_{x\in\mathfrak{B}}\left(\sum_{i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}}\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|h_{i,t}(x)|\right)\cdot|\psi_{i,j}^{(t)}(\bm{X}_{n,s})|\cdot\sum_{r=1}^{L}(\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)})^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\widetilde{O}(\Xi_{2})\max_{i^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{M}_{j}}|\langle w_{i^{\prime}}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{\bm{X}_{n,s}\in\mathfrak{N}}\frac{1}{\tau}\int_{0}^{1}\widehat{\ell}^{\prime}_{s,t}(\nu)d\nu\cdot|\psi_{i,j}^{(t)}(\bm{X}_{n,s})|\cdot\sum_{r=1}^{L}(\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)})^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=R_{1,1}+R_{1,2}+\frac{1}{d^{\Omega(\log d)}}

where for
\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{1}}⃝
and
\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{2}}⃝
, we argue as follows:

for
\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{1}}⃝
, we used the fact that the expectations over
s\in\mathfrak{N}
in the summation can be viewed as independently and uniformly selecting from
s\in\mathfrak{N}
, which allows us to equate
\sum_{X\in\mathfrak{N}}=|\mathfrak{N}|
.

for
\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{2}}⃝
, we use Fact
E.1
to ensure that
\sum_{i\in[m]}\bm{1}_{h_{i,t}(\bm{X}_{n})\neq 0}\leq\widetilde{O}(\Xi_{2})
with high probability.

we have for any
x\in\mathfrak{B}
:

|\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n})-f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}^{\setminus j}),f_{t}(x)\rangle|\leq\sum_{i^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{j}}|\langle w_{i^{\prime}}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|\cdot|h_{i^{\prime},t}(x)|+\widetilde{O}(\Xi_{2})\max_{i^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{j}}|\langle w_{i^{\prime}}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|

(327)

which gives the desired inequality.

Now we proceed to deal with
R_{1,1}
, since
i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}^{\star}
; we have automatically
\bm{1}_{h_{i,t}(\bm{X}_{n,s})\neq 0}=\bm{1}_{|\hat{z}_{n,s,j}|\neq 0}
w.h.p., so we can transform
R_{1,1}
as

\displaystyle R_{1,1}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{|\mathfrak{N}|}{\tau}\int_{0}^{1}\widehat{\ell}^{\prime}_{s,t}(\nu)d\nu\max_{x\in\mathfrak{B}}\left(\sum_{i^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{j}}\langle w_{i^{\prime}}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|h_{i^{\prime},t}(x)|\right)|\psi_{i,j}^{(t)}(\bm{X}_{n,s})|\sum_{r=1}^{L}(\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)})^{2}\right]+\frac{1}{d^{\Omega(\log d)}}

(328)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{|\mathfrak{B}|}{\tau}\int_{0}^{1}\widehat{\ell}_{s,t}(\nu)d\nu\left(\sum_{i^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{j}}\langle w_{i^{\prime}}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle^{2}+\Upsilon_{j}^{(t)}\right)|\psi_{i,j}^{(t)}(\bm{X}_{n,s})|\sum_{r=1}^{L}(\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)})^{2}\right]+\frac{1}{d^{\Omega(\log d)}}

where
\Upsilon_{j}^{(t)}
is defined as:

\Upsilon_{j}^{(t)}:=\max_{x\in\mathfrak{B}}\left(\sum_{i^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{j}}|h_{i^{\prime},t}(x)|\cdot|\langle w_{i^{\prime}}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|-\sum_{i^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{j}}\langle w_{i^{\prime}}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle^{2}\right)

(329)

We proceed to give a high probability bound for

\sum_{i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}}|h_{i,t}(\bm{X}_{n,s})|\cdot|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|
,
which lies in the core of our proof. In order to apply Lemma
E.7
. to the pre-activation in
h_{i,t}(\bm{X}_{n,s})
, one can first expand as

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in[m]}|h_{i,t}(\bm{X}_{n,s})|\cdot|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|

(330)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{j^{\prime}\in[d]}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{M}_{j^{\prime}}}|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle|\cdot\sum_{q=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}_{n,s,j^{\prime}}^{(q)}|\cdot|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|+\sum_{i\in[m]}\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{d}}\right)|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|

\displaystyle=\sum_{j^{\prime}\in[d]}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{M}_{j^{\prime}}\cap\mathcal{M}_{j}}\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\sum_{q=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}_{n,s,j^{\prime}}^{(q)}|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|

\displaystyle+\sum_{j^{\prime}\in[d]}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{M}_{j^{\prime}}\setminus\mathcal{M}_{j}}|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle|\cdot\sum_{q=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}_{n,s,j^{\prime}}^{(q)}|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|+\sum_{i\in[m]}\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{d}}\right)|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\leq}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}}\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle^{2}\sum_{q=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}_{n,s,j^{\prime}}^{(q)}|+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j^{\prime}\neq j\\
i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}\cap\mathcal{M}_{j^{\prime}}\end{subarray}}|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle|\cdot\sum_{q=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}_{n,s,j^{\prime}}^{(q)}|\cdot|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|

\displaystyle+\sum_{j^{\prime}\in[d]}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{M}_{j^{\prime}}\setminus\mathcal{M}_{j}}|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle|\cdot\sum_{q=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}_{n,s,j^{\prime}}^{(q)}|\cdot|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|+\sum_{i\in[m]}\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{d}}\right)|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|

And we proceed to calculate the last two terms on the RHS as follows: Firstly, from Lemma
J.1
. We know for the set of neurons
\Gamma_{j}:=\{j^{\prime}\neq j,\ j^{\prime}\in[d]:\mathcal{M}_{j}\cap\mathcal{M}_{j^{\prime}}\neq\emptyset\}
, we have
|\Gamma_{j}|\leq O(\log d)
, and

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{j^{\prime}\neq j}\sum_{i^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{j}\cap\mathcal{M}_{j^{\prime}}}|\langle w_{i^{\prime}}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle|\cdot\sum_{q=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}_{n,s,j^{\prime}}^{(q)}|\cdot|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|\right|

(331)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O\left(\frac{\log d}{\sqrt{\Xi_{2}}}\right)\cdot\sum_{j^{\prime}\in\Gamma_{j}}O(\tau\log^{2}d)\sum_{q=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}_{n,s,j^{\prime}}^{(q)}|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O\left(\frac{\tau}{\log d}\right)

where in the last inequality we have taken into account that
\mathbb{E}[\sum_{q=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}_{n,s,j}^{(q)}|]=\widetilde{O}(1/d)
and have used Lemma
E.8
. The same techniques also provide the following bound:

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{j^{\prime}\in[d]}\sum_{i^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{j^{\prime}}\setminus\mathcal{M}_{j}}|\langle w_{i^{\prime}}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle|\cdot\sum_{q=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}_{n,s,j^{\prime}}^{(q)}|\cdot|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|\right|

\displaystyle\leq O\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}}\right)\sum_{j^{\prime}\neq j}O(\Xi_{2})\sum_{q=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}_{n,s,j^{\prime}}^{(q)}|

(332)

\displaystyle\leq O\left(\frac{\Xi_{2}^{2}}{\sqrt{d}}\right)

Therefore via a union bound, we have

\sum_{i^{\prime}\in[m]}|h_{i^{\prime},t}(\bm{X}_{n,s})|\cdot|\langle w_{i^{\prime}}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|\leq\sum_{i^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{j}}\langle w_{i^{\prime}}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle^{2}\sum_{q=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}_{n,s,j^{\prime}}^{(q)}|+O\left(\frac{\tau}{\log d}\right)

(333)

The same arguments also gives ( + further applying Lemma
E.7
.)

\max_{x\in\{\bm{X}_{n},\bm{Y}_{n}\}}\left\{\sum_{i^{\prime}\in[m]}|h_{i^{\prime},t}(x)|\cdot|\langle w_{i^{\prime}}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|\right\}\leq\sum_{i^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{j}}\langle w_{i^{\prime}}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle^{2}\sum_{r=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j^{\prime}}|+O\left(\frac{\tau}{\log d}\right)

(334)

which also implies that

\Upsilon_{j}^{(t)}\leq\max_{x\in\mathfrak{B}}\sum_{i^{\prime}\in[m]}|h_{i,t}(x)|\cdot|\langle w_{i^{\prime}}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|-\sum_{i^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{j}}|\langle w_{i^{\prime}}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|^{2}\sum_{r=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j^{\prime}}|\leq O\left(\frac{\tau}{\log d}\right)

(335)

Now we are ready to control the quantity
R_{1,1}
. The idea here is to ”decorrelate” the factor
\widehat{\ell}_{s,t}^{\prime}(\nu)
from the others. Defining

\mathfrak{B}\setminus^{s}:=\{\bm{Y}_{n}\}\cup\mathfrak{N}\setminus\{\bm{X}_{n,s}\},\quad\text{and}\quad\mathfrak{B}_{s}^{\prime}:=\mathfrak{B}\setminus^{s}\cup\{\bm{X}_{n,s}^{\setminus j}\},

(336)

there exists a constant
G_{1}^{\prime}>0
such that, if

\sum_{i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}}\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle^{2}(\sum_{r=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(r)})^{2}\leq(\frac{\epsilon_{j}}{\epsilon_{\max}})^{2}\tau G_{1}^{\prime}\log d,

(337)

we have w.h.p.

\displaystyle\frac{\ell_{s,t}^{\prime}(\bm{X}_{n},\mathfrak{B})}{\ell_{s,t}^{\prime}(\bm{X}_{n},\mathfrak{B}_{s}^{\prime})}

\displaystyle=\frac{e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n,s})\rangle/\tau}}{e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n,s}^{\setminus j})\rangle/\tau}}\cdot\frac{\sum_{x\in\mathfrak{B}_{s}^{\prime}}e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(x)\rangle/\tau}}{\sum_{x\in\mathfrak{B}}e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(x)\rangle/\tau}}

(338)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{e^{2\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n,s})\rangle/\tau}}{e^{2\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n,s}^{\setminus j})\rangle/\tau}}

\displaystyle\leq e^{2(\frac{\epsilon_{j}}{\epsilon_{\max}})^{2}G_{1}^{\prime}\log d+O(1/\log d)}\leq o\left(\frac{d}{\Xi_{2}^{5}}\right)

Now we define

\mathfrak{N}_{s}^{\prime}:=\{X_{n,s^{\prime}}\in\mathfrak{N}\setminus\{\bm{X}_{n,s}\}:\sum_{q=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,u,j}^{(q)}=0\}\cup\{X^{\setminus j}_{n,s}\}.

(339)

Note that from concentration inequality of Bernoulli variables, we know

|\mathfrak{N}_{s}^{\prime}|=\Omega(|\mathfrak{N}|)

(340)

Thus we have (notice that the outer factor
|\mathfrak{N}|
can be inserted into the expectation by sacrificing some constant factors):

\displaystyle R_{1,1}

\displaystyle\leq O\left(\frac{|\mathfrak{N}|}{\tau}\right)\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[\widehat{\ell}_{s,t}(1)\left(\sum_{i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}}\langle w_{i^{\prime}}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle^{2}+\Upsilon_{j}^{(t)}\right)\cdot|\psi_{i,j}^{(t)}(\bm{X}_{n,s})|\cdot\sum_{r=1}^{L}(\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)})^{2}\right]

(341)

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{|\mathfrak{N}|\log\log d}{d\tau}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\ell_{s,t}^{\prime}(\bm{X}_{n},\mathfrak{B})\left(\sum_{i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}}\langle w_{i^{\prime}}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle^{2}+\Upsilon_{j}^{(t)}\right)\cdot|\psi_{i,j}^{(t)}(\bm{X}_{n,s})|\,\Big|\,\sum_{r=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}|\neq 0\right]

\displaystyle=\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{|\mathfrak{N}|}{d\tau}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\ell_{s,t}^{\prime}(\bm{X}_{n},\mathfrak{B})}{\ell_{s,t}^{\prime}(\bm{X}_{n},\mathfrak{B}_{s}^{\prime})}\cdot\ell_{s,t}^{\prime}(\bm{X}_{n},\mathfrak{B}_{s}^{\prime})\left(\sum_{i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}}\langle w_{i^{\prime}}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle^{2}+\Upsilon_{j}^{(t)}\right)\,\Big|\,|\psi_{i,j}^{(t)}(\bm{X}_{n,s})|,\sum_{r=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}|\neq 0\right]

\displaystyle\leq O\left(\frac{1}{\tau\Xi_{2}^{4}}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{\bm{X}_{n,s}\in\mathfrak{N}^{\prime}}\ell_{s,t}^{\prime}(\bm{X}_{n},\mathfrak{B}_{s}^{\prime})\left(\sum_{i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}}\langle w_{i^{\prime}}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle^{2}+\Upsilon_{j}^{(t)}\right)\cdot|\psi_{i,j}^{(t)}(\bm{X}_{n,s})|\,\Big|\,\sum_{r=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}|\neq 0\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\text{\textcircled{\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{1}}}}}{\leq}\sum_{q=1}^{L}|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\tilde{z}_{n,s,j}^{(q)}-b_{i}^{(t)}|\cdot O\left(\frac{1}{\Xi_{2}^{3}}\right)\cdot\bm{Pr}(\sum_{q=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}_{n,s,j}^{(q)}|\neq 0)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{r=1}^{L}|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j}|\cdot O\left(\frac{1}{\Xi_{2}^{3}}\right)\cdot\bm{Pr}(\sum_{r=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j}|\neq 0)

Where in inequality
\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{1}}⃝
, we have used the independence of
\sum_{q=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}_{n,s,j}^{(q)}|
with respect to
\ell_{s,t}^{\prime}(\bm{X}_{n},\mathfrak{B}_{s}^{\prime})
, and the fact that

\sum_{\bm{X}_{n,s}\in\mathfrak{N}}\ell_{s,t}^{\prime}(\bm{X}_{n},\mathfrak{B}_{s}^{\prime})\leq 1

(342)

Now turn back to deal with
R_{1,2}
(by Newton-Leibniz). Indeed, noticing that

\max_{i^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{M}_{j}}|\langle w_{i^{\prime}}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|\leq O\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}}\right)\quad\text{(from Lemma~\ref{Theorem E.1})},

(343)

and that

\sum_{x\in\mathfrak{N}}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{\tau}\int_{0}^{1}\widehat{\ell}_{s,t}^{\prime}(\nu)\,\mathrm{d}\nu\cdot|\psi_{i,j}^{(t)}(\bm{X}_{n,s})|\cdot\sum_{r=1}^{L}(\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)})^{2}\right]\leq\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{1}{d}\right)\cdot\sum_{r=1}^{L}|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j}-b_{i}^{(t)}|

(344)

We have
R_{1,2}\leq o(R_{1,1})
. For
R_{2}
(in By Newton-Leibniz), we can see from the definition of

\ell_{s,t}^{\prime}(\bm{X}_{n}^{\setminus j},\mathfrak{B})

(345)

that it is independent of
\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}
. Notice further that

\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle\geq b_{i}^{(t)}}=\bm{1}_{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\neq 0}

(346)

with high probability due to our assumption, and also the fact that
\bm{1}_{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\neq 0}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}
has mean zero and is independent of
\ell_{s,t}^{\prime}(\bm{X}_{n}^{\setminus j},\bm{X}_{n,s})
, we have

\displaystyle R_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{poly}(d)\cdot e^{-\Omega(\log^{2}d)}

(347)

\displaystyle=d^{O(1)}\cdot e^{-\Omega(\log^{2}d)}

\displaystyle=d^{O(1)}\cdot(e^{\log d})^{-\Omega(\log d)}

\displaystyle=d^{O(1)}\cdot d^{-\Omega(\log d)}

\displaystyle=d^{-\Omega(\log d)}\lesssim\frac{1}{\operatorname{poly}(d)^{\Omega(\log d)}}

Combining the pieces above together, we can have

\Psi_{i,j,2}^{(t)}\leq O\left(\frac{1}{\Xi_{2}^{3}}\mathbb{E}[(\sum_{r=1}^{L}\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j})^{2}]\right)\cdot\sum_{r=1}^{L}|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j}-b_{i}^{(t)}|

(348)

Now we turn to
\Psi_{i,j,1}^{(t)}(j)
, whose calculation is similar. Defining

\mathfrak{B}_{j}:=\{\sum_{r=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j^{\prime}}|=0,\ \forall j^{\prime}\neq j\},

(349)

we separately discuss the cases when events
\mathfrak{B}_{j}
or
\mathfrak{B}_{j}^{c}
hold:

When
\mathfrak{B}_{j}^{c}
happens,

\mathrm{sign}(\psi_{i,j}^{(t)}(\bm{Y}_{n}))=\mathrm{sign}(\sum_{s=1}^{L}\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)})

(350)

with high probability by Fact
E.1
since we assumed
i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}^{\star}
. Thus, if
\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle>0
, we have

\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\ell_{p,t}^{\prime}-1\right)\psi_{i,j}^{(t)}(\bm{Y}_{n})\cdot\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\,\middle|\,\mathfrak{B}_{j}^{c}\right]\geq 0

(351)

If
\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle<0
, the opposite inequality holds as well.

When
\mathfrak{B}_{j}
happens, it is easy to derive that

\displaystyle|\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(\bm{Y}_{n})\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{j}}\langle w_{i^{\prime}}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle^{2}(\sum_{s=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)})^{2}+O(\Xi_{2})\cdot O\left(\frac{\|w_{i^{\prime}}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{d}}\right)

(352)

\displaystyle\leq\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle^{2}(\sum_{s=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)})^{2}+O\left(\frac{\Xi_{2}}{\sqrt{d}}\right)

and therefore

\displaystyle|\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(\bm{Y}_{n})\rangle-\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}^{\setminus j}),f_{t}(\bm{Y}_{n}^{\setminus j})\rangle|

(353)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{j}}\langle w_{i^{\prime}}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle^{2}(\sum_{s=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)})^{2}+O\left(\frac{\Xi_{2}}{\sqrt{d}}\right)

From previous analysis, we also have

\displaystyle|\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n,s})\rangle-\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}^{\setminus j}),f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n,s})\rangle|

(354)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{j}}\langle w_{i^{\prime}}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle^{2}(\sum_{r=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)})^{2}+O\left(\frac{\tau}{\log d}\right)+O\left(\frac{\Xi_{2}}{\sqrt{d}}\right)

These inequalities allow us to apply the same techniques in bounding
\Phi_{1,2}^{(t)}
as follows. We define
\mathfrak{B}_{p}^{\prime}:=\mathfrak{N}\cup\{\bm{Y}_{n}^{\setminus j}\}
.
Then, for some
G_{2}^{\prime}=\Theta(1)
, we can have:

\displaystyle\frac{\ell_{p,t}^{\prime}(\bm{X}_{n}^{\setminus j},\mathfrak{B})}{\ell_{p,t}^{\prime}(\bm{X}_{n}^{\setminus j},\mathfrak{B}_{p}^{\prime})}

\displaystyle=\frac{e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(\bm{Y}_{n})\rangle/\tau}}{e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}^{\setminus j}),f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}^{\setminus j})\rangle/\tau}}\cdot\frac{\sum_{x\in\mathfrak{B}_{p}^{\prime}}e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}^{\setminus j}),f_{t}(x)\rangle/\tau}}{\sum_{x\in\mathfrak{B}}e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(x)\rangle/\tau}}

(355)

\displaystyle\leq e^{2(\frac{\epsilon_{j}}{\epsilon_{\max}})^{2}G_{2}^{\prime}\log d+O(1/\log d)}\leq O\left(\frac{d}{\Xi_{2}^{5}}\right)

Now we can proceed to compute as follows:
