\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\ell_{p,t}^{\prime}(\bm{X}_{n},\mathfrak{B})\cdot\psi_{i,j}^{(t)}(\bm{Y}_{n})\cdot\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\,\middle|\,\mathfrak{B}_{j}\right]

(356)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\ell_{p,t}^{\prime}(\bm{X}_{n}^{\setminus j},\mathfrak{B})}{\ell_{p,t}^{\prime}(\bm{X}_{n}^{\setminus j},\mathfrak{B}_{p}^{\prime})}\cdot\ell_{p,t}^{\prime}(\bm{X}_{n}^{\setminus j},\mathfrak{B}_{p}^{\prime})\cdot\psi_{i,j}^{(t)}(\bm{Y}_{n})\cdot\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\,\middle|\,\mathfrak{B}_{j}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{L}|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}-b_{i}^{(t)}|\cdot O\left(\frac{1}{\Xi_{2}^{5}}\right)\cdot\bm{Pr}(\sum_{s=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}|\neq 0)

But from Lemma
E.10
, and the fact that Lemma
D.1
still holds for Stage III, we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\psi_{i,j}^{(t)}(\bm{Y}_{n})\cdot\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\cdot\bm{1}_{\mathfrak{B}_{j}}\right]

(357)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{sign}(\sum_{s=1}^{L}\left(\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}\right))\cdot(\sum_{s=1}^{L}|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}-b_{i}^{(t)}|)\cdot\frac{1}{\operatorname{polylog}(d)}\cdot\mathbb{E}[\sum_{s=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}|]

Combining both cases above gives the bound of
\Psi_{i,j,1}^{(t)}
. Combining results for
\Psi_{i,j,1}^{(t)}
and
\Psi_{i,j,2}^{(t)}
concludes the proof. The constant
G_{1}
in the statement can be defined as:

G_{1}:=\min\{G_{1}^{\prime},G_{2}^{\prime}\}.

(358)

∎

J.3
Proof of Lemma
E.3
:

Proof of Lemma
E.3
:

First we deal with the case of
i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}^{\star}
, we have

\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}^{(t)}}\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j}=\bm{1}_{\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j}\neq 0}\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j}

(359)

when conditions in Lemma
E.1
hold. Now by denoting

\displaystyle\widetilde{\psi}_{i,j}^{(t)}(\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+}):=

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{L}\left(\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}-b_{i}^{(t)}\right)\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}>0}

(360)

\displaystyle-\left(\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}+b_{i}^{(t)}\right)\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}<0}

we can then easily rewrite
\Psi_{i,j}^{(t)}
as (by using Fact
E.1
)

\displaystyle\Psi_{i,j}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1-\ell_{p,t}^{\prime}\right)\widetilde{\psi}_{i,j}^{(t)}(\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+})-\sum_{\bm{X}_{n,s}\in\mathfrak{N}}\ell_{s,t}^{\prime}\cdot\widetilde{\psi}_{i,j}^{(t)}(\tilde{z}_{n,s,j})\right]\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j}\neq 0}\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j}+\frac{1}{\operatorname{poly}(d)^{\Omega(\log d)}}

(361)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\bm{X}_{n},\bm{Y}_{n}}\left[\left(\widetilde{\psi}_{i,j}^{(t)}(\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+})-I_{1}-I_{2}\right)\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j}\neq 0}\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j}\right]+\frac{1}{\operatorname{poly}(d)^{\Omega(\log d)}}

where
I_{1}
and
I_{2}
are defined as follows:

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle:=\mathbb{E}_{\mathfrak{N}}\left[(\ell_{p,t}^{\prime})\widetilde{\psi}_{i,j}^{(t)}(\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+})+\sum_{\bm{X}_{n,s}\in\mathfrak{N}}\ell_{s,t}^{\prime}\cdot\widetilde{\psi}_{i,j}^{(t)}(\tilde{z}_{n,s,j})\bm{1}_{\tilde{z}_{n,s,j}=\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+}}\right]

(362)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{\textcircled{\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{1}}}}}{=}\widetilde{\psi}_{i,j}^{(t)}(\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+})\mathbb{E}_{\mathfrak{N}}\left[\frac{|\mathfrak{N}|e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n,s})\rangle/\tau}\bm{1}_{\tilde{z}_{n,s,j}=\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+}}+e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(\bm{Y}_{n})\rangle/\tau}}{e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n,s})\rangle/\tau}+\sum_{x\in\mathfrak{N}\setminus\{\bm{X}_{n,s}\}}e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(x)\rangle/\tau}}\right]

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle:=\mathbb{E}_{\mathfrak{N}}\left[\sum_{\bm{X}_{n,s}\in\mathfrak{N}}\ell_{s,t}^{\prime}\cdot\widetilde{\psi}_{i,j}^{(t)}(\tilde{z}_{n,s,j})\bm{1}_{\tilde{z}_{n,s,j}\neq\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+}}\right]

where in
\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{1}}⃝
we used the identification
\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+}=\tilde{z}_{n,s,j}
.
The tricky part here is since all the variables inside the expectation is non-negative, we can use Jensen’s inequality to move the expectation of
e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(X_{n,u})\rangle/\tau}
to the denominator.
We let

V:=e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(\bm{Y}_{n})\rangle/\tau}

(363)

and consider it fixed when computing
I_{1}
as follows: conditioned on
\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+}\neq 0
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{I_{1}}{\widetilde{\psi}_{i,j}^{(t)}(\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+})}

(364)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathfrak{N}}\left[\frac{|\mathfrak{N}|\cdot e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n,s})\rangle/\tau}\bm{1}_{\tilde{z}_{n,s,j}=\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+}}+V}{e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n,s})\rangle/\tau}+V+\sum_{x\in\mathfrak{N}\setminus\{\bm{X}_{n,s}\}}e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(X_{n,u})\rangle/\tau}}\right]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\bm{X}_{n,s}}\left[\frac{e^{\langle f_{t}(X_{p}^{+}),f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n,s})\rangle/\tau}\cdot\bm{1}_{\tilde{z}_{n,s,j}=\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+}}+\frac{1}{|\mathfrak{N}|}V}{\frac{1}{|\mathfrak{N}|}e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n,s})\rangle/\tau}+\frac{1}{|\mathfrak{N}|}V+\frac{|\mathfrak{N}|-1}{|\mathfrak{N}|}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{X}_{n}}\left[e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n})\rangle/\tau}\right]}\right]\quad\text{(by Jensen inequality)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\bm{X}_{n,s}}\left[\frac{e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n,s})\rangle/\tau}\cdot\bm{1}_{\tilde{z}_{n,s,j}=\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+}}+\frac{1}{|\mathfrak{N}|}V}{\frac{1}{|\mathfrak{N}|}\left(e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n,s})\rangle/\tau}+V\right)+\frac{|\mathfrak{N}|-1}{|\mathfrak{N}|}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{X}_{n}}\left[e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n})\rangle/\tau}\left(\bm{1}_{\tilde{z}_{n,s,j}=\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+}}+\bm{1}_{\tilde{z}_{n,s,j}\neq\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+}}\right)\right]}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\text{\textcircled{\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{1}}}}}{\geq}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\bm{X}_{n,s}}\left[\frac{X+\frac{1}{|\mathfrak{N}|}V}{\frac{1}{|\mathfrak{N}|}(X+V)+\frac{|\mathfrak{N}|-1}{|\mathfrak{N}|}\left(1+\frac{1}{\operatorname{poly}(d)}\right)\mathbb{E}_{\bm{X}_{n}}[X]}\right]

\displaystyle\text{where}\quad X:=e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n})\rangle/\tau}\cdot\bm{1}_{\tilde{z}_{n,s,j}=\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+}}\geq 0)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{\textcircled{\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{2}}}}}{\geq}

\displaystyle 1-O\left(\frac{1}{\operatorname{poly}(d)}\right)

•

in
\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{1}}⃝
, we need to go through similar analysis as in the proof of Lemma
E.2
to obtain that, with high probability over
\bm{X}_{n}
and
\bm{X}_{n}^{\setminus j}
:

\displaystyle\frac{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n})\rangle-\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}^{\setminus j})\rangle}{\tau}

(365)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}}\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle^{2}(\sum_{s=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)})^{2}-O\left(\frac{1}{\log d}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle G_{2}(\frac{\epsilon_{j}}{\epsilon_{\max}})^{2}\log d-O\left(\frac{1}{\log d}\right)

for some very large constant
G_{2}=\Theta(1)
, which gives (the
\frac{1}{\operatorname{poly}(d)}
here depends on how large
G_{2}
is):

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\bm{X}_{n}}\left[e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n})\rangle/\tau}\cdot\bm{1}_{\tilde{z}_{n,s,j}\neq\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+}}\right]

(366)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\operatorname{poly}(d)}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{X}_{n}}\left[e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n})\rangle/\tau}\cdot\bm{1}_{\tilde{z}_{n,s,j}=\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+}}\right]

•

in inequality
\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{2}}⃝
, we need to argue as follows, where

\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}[X]\overset{\text{abbr.}}{=}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{X}_{n}}[X]

(367)

is only integrated over the randomness of
\bm{X}_{n}
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\bm{X}_{n,s}}\left[\frac{X+\frac{1}{|\mathfrak{N}|}V}{\frac{1}{|\mathfrak{N}|}(X+V)+\frac{|\mathfrak{N}|-1}{|\mathfrak{N}|}\left(1+\frac{1}{\operatorname{poly}(d)}\right)\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}[X]}\right]

(368)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 1-\frac{1}{|\mathfrak{N}|}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{X}_{n,s}}\left[\frac{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}[X]}{\frac{1}{|\mathfrak{N}|}(X+V)+\frac{|\mathfrak{N}|-1}{|\mathfrak{N}|}\left(1+\frac{1}{\operatorname{poly}(d)}\right)\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}[X]}\right]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 1-\frac{1}{|\mathfrak{N}|}\cdot\frac{\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}[X]}{\left(\frac{|\mathfrak{N}|-1}{|\mathfrak{N}|}\left(1+\frac{1}{\operatorname{poly}(d)}\right)\widetilde{\mathbb{E}}[X]\right)}\quad\text{(since }X+V\geq 0\text{)}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 1-\frac{1}{\operatorname{poly}(d)}

The same analysis applies to
I_{2}
, which we can bound as

\left|\frac{I_{2}}{\widetilde{\psi}_{i,j}^{(t)}(-\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+})}\right|\leq\frac{1}{\operatorname{poly}(d)}

(369)

Combining both
I_{1}
and
I_{2}
, we have

\Psi_{i,j}^{(t)}\leq\frac{1}{\operatorname{poly}(d)}\sum_{s=1}\left|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}\right|

(370)

In the case of
i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}
, we have with probability
\leq\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{1}{d}\right)
that

\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle\geq b_{i}^{(t)}}\neq\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j}>0}\quad\text{or}\quad\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},z^{(s)}_{Y}\rangle>b_{i}^{(t)}}\neq\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}>0}

(371)

When such events happen, we can obtain a bound of

O\left(\frac{1}{d}\right)b_{i}^{(t)}

(372)

over
\Psi_{i,j}^{(t)}
, which times the probability
\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{1}{d}\right)
leads to our bound.

Combining the above observations and the analyses, we can complete the proof.
∎

J.4
Proof of Lemma
E.4
:

Proof of Lemma
E.4
:

Let
j\in[d]
, since the case of
j\in[d_{1}]\setminus[d]
can be similarly dealt with. We first look at the following
\mathcal{E}_{1,i,j}^{(t)}
term in
\mathcal{E}_{i,j}^{(t)}
:

\mathcal{E}_{1,i,j}^{(t)}=\mathbb{E}\left[h_{i,t}(\bm{Y}_{n})\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\langle\bm{M}_{j},\tilde{\xi}^{(r)}_{n}\rangle\right]

(373)

we have

|\mathcal{E}_{1,i,j}^{(t)}|=\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}|\langle w^{(t)}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle|\bm{1}_{|\langle w^{(t)}_{i},\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle|\geq b^{(t)}_{i}+|\langle w^{(t)}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}-\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle|}|\langle\bm{M}_{j},\tilde{\xi}^{(r)}_{n}\rangle|\right]

(374)

When
\left\{|\langle w^{(t)}_{i},\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle|\geq b^{(t)}_{i}+|\langle w^{(t)}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}-\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle|\right\}
happens (which we know from Fact
E.1
has prob
\leq\widetilde{O}(\frac{1}{d})
), using Lemma
E.7
, we have

|\mathcal{E}_{1,i,j}^{(t)}|\leq\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{1}{d^{2}}\right)\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}

(375)

Proof of
\mathcal{E}_{2,i,j}^{(t)}
can refer to Lemma E.7 and Lemma E.8 in
(
Wen & Li 2021
)
.

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{2,i,j}^{(t)}=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{\bm{X}_{n,s}\in\mathfrak{N}}\ell_{s,t}^{\prime}(\bm{X}_{n},\mathfrak{B})\cdot h_{i,t}(\bm{X}_{n,s})\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\langle\bm{M}_{j},\tilde{\xi}^{(r)}_{n}\rangle\right]

(376)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O\left(\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}\Xi_{2}^{2}}{d^{2}\tau}\right)\cdot\max_{i^{\prime}\in[m]}\left(\left|\langle w_{i^{\prime}}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\right|\right)

Combining the results of
\mathcal{E}_{1,i,j}^{(t)}
and
\mathcal{E}_{2,i,j}^{(t)}
, we can conclude the proof.
∎

J.5
Proof of Lemma
E.5
:

Proof of Lemma
E.5
:

The proof essentially relies on the condition that Lemma
E.1
holds for all
t^{\prime}\in[T_{3},t]
. We first consider the case where
i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}^{\star}
. Similar to how
\psi_{i,j}^{(t)}(x)
are defined for each
x
in Definition
E.1
, for each
j^{\prime}\neq j
, we let

\rho_{i,j}^{(t)}(\bm{Y}_{n}):=\sum_{s=1}^{L}\left(\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},z^{(s)}_{Y}\rangle-\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}\tilde{z}_{n}^{+(s)}\rangle\right)\bm{1}_{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}\neq 0}

(377)

Now it is straightforward to decompose
\Phi_{i,j}^{(t)}
as follows:

\displaystyle\Phi_{i,j}^{(t)}

(378)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left((\ell^{\prime}_{p,t}-1)\cdot\phi_{i,j}^{(t)}(\bm{Y}_{n})+\sum_{\bm{X}_{n,s}\in\mathfrak{N}}\ell^{\prime}_{s,t}\cdot\phi_{i,j}^{(t)}(\bm{X}_{n,s})\right)\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j^{\prime}\in[d],j^{\prime}\neq j}\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\mathbb{E}\left[\left((\ell^{\prime}_{p,t}-1)\cdot\sum_{s=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,j^{\prime}}^{+(s)}+\sum_{\bm{X}_{n,s}\in\mathfrak{N}}\ell^{\prime}_{s,t}\cdot\sum_{q=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,s,j^{\prime}}^{(q)}\bm{1}_{\tilde{z}_{n,s,j^{\prime}}^{(q)}\neq 0}\right)\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]\quad

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left((\ell^{\prime}_{p,t}-1)\cdot\rho_{i,j}^{(t)}(\bm{Y}_{n})+\sum_{\bm{X}_{n,s}\in\mathfrak{N}}\ell^{\prime}_{s,t}\cdot\rho_{i,j}^{(t)}(\bm{X}_{n,s})\right)\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]+\frac{1}{\operatorname{poly}(d)^{\Omega(\log d)}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle H_{1}+H_{2}+\frac{1}{\operatorname{poly}(d)^{\Omega(\log d)}}

Indeed, from similar arguments as in the proof of Lemma
E.3
and Lemma
E.4
, we can trivially obtain

|H_{2}|\leq O\left(\frac{\Xi_{2}^{2}}{d^{2}}\right)\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}

(379)

Now we turn to
H_{1}
. Since
\max_{j^{\prime}\neq j}|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle|\leq O\left(\frac{\epsilon_{j}}{\epsilon_{\max}}\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{d}\Xi_{2}^{5}}\right)
, we can simply get (Since w.h.p.,
|\{j^{\prime}\in[d]:\tilde{z}_{n,j^{\prime}}^{+(s)}\neq 0\}|=\widetilde{O}(1)
, and if
\tilde{z}_{n,j^{\prime}}^{+(s)}=0
, the negative terms are small from similar analysis in Lemma
E.2
)

\displaystyle|H_{1}|

(380)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O\left(\frac{\epsilon_{j}}{\epsilon_{\max}}\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{d}\Xi_{2}^{5}}\right)\sum_{j^{\prime}\neq j,\,j^{\prime}\in[d]}\mathbb{E}\left[\left((\ell_{p,t}^{\prime}-1)\cdot\sum_{s=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,j^{\prime}}^{+(s)}+\sum_{\bm{X}_{n,s}\in\mathfrak{N}}\ell_{s,t}^{\prime}\cdot\sum_{q=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,s,j^{\prime}}^{(q)}\bm{1}_{\tilde{z}_{n,s,j^{\prime}}^{(q)}\neq 0}\right)\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O\left(\frac{\epsilon_{j}}{\epsilon_{\max}}\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{d^{3/2}}\right)

Then we can obtain a crude bound for all
t\in\left[\frac{d^{1.01}}{\eta},\frac{d^{1.99}}{\eta}\right]
by

\Phi_{i,j}^{(t)}\leq(H_{1}+H_{2})+\frac{1}{\operatorname{poly}(d)^{\Omega(\log d)}}\leq\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\epsilon_{j}}{\epsilon_{\max}}\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{d^{3/2}}\right)

(381)

The harder part is to deal with iterations
t\in\left[\frac{d^{1.495}}{\eta},\frac{d^{1.498}}{\eta}\right]
.
We first establish a connection between
\Psi^{(t)}
and
\Phi^{(t)}
.
We first assume that for all
j^{\prime}\neq j,j^{\prime}\in[d]
, it holds that

\left|\Psi_{i^{\prime},j^{\prime}}^{(t_{1})}\right|/\left|\langle w_{i^{\prime}}^{(t_{1})},\bm{M}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\right|\leq\Omega\left(\frac{\Xi^{2}_{2}}{\sqrt{d}t\eta}\right),

(382)

which is true for all iteration
t\leq\frac{d\,\operatorname{polylog}(d)}{\eta}
from simple calculations.

Now suppose at some
t_{1}\geq\frac{d\,\operatorname{polylog}(d)}{\eta}
, there exists some
j^{\prime}\neq j,j^{\prime}\in[d]
and
i^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{j}^{\star}
such that

\left|\Psi_{i^{\prime},j^{\prime}}^{(t_{1})}\right|/\left|\langle w_{i^{\prime}}^{(t_{1})},\bm{M}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\right|\geq\Omega\left(\frac{\Xi_{2}}{\sqrt{d}t\eta}\right)

(383)

which means we have the followings:

\mathbb{E}\left[\left((\ell_{p,t_{1}}^{\prime}-1)\sum_{s=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,j^{\prime}}^{+(s)}+\sum_{\bm{X}_{n,s}\in\mathfrak{N}}\ell_{s,t_{1}}^{\prime}\sum_{q=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,s,j^{\prime}}^{(q)}\right)\sum_{r=1}^{L}\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j^{\prime}}\right]\geq\Omega\left(\frac{\Xi_{2}}{\sqrt{d}t\eta}\right)

(384)

Letting
\Delta>0
be defined as the number such that if
\mathfrak{F}_{j}^{(t)}=\Delta
, we can have

\left|\Psi_{i^{\prime},j^{\prime}}^{(t)}\right|/\left|\langle w_{i^{\prime}}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\right|\leq O\left(\frac{\sqrt{\Xi_{2}}\tau\log d}{\sqrt{dt\eta}}\right).

(385)

Then from the calculations in the proof of Lemma
E.2
, there must be a constant

\delta>\Omega(1)
such that

\mathfrak{F}_{j}^{(t_{1})}\geq\Delta-\delta\tau\log d.

(386)

However, such growth cannot continue since for some
t^{\prime}=\Theta(t/\sqrt{\Xi_{2}}),\text{ we have for each }i^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{j}^{\prime}
:

\displaystyle\left|\langle w_{i^{\prime}}^{(t_{1}+t^{\prime})},\bm{M}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\geq\left|\langle w_{i^{\prime}}^{(t_{1}+t^{\prime})},\bm{M}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\right|(1-\eta\lambda)+\Psi_{i^{\prime},j^{\prime}}^{(t_{1}+t^{\prime}-1)}+\Phi_{i^{\prime},j^{\prime}}^{(t)}+O\left(\frac{\Xi_{2}^{2}}{d^{2}}\right)

(387)

\displaystyle\geq\left|\langle w_{i^{\prime}}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\right|(1-\eta\lambda)^{t^{\prime}}+\sum_{s=t}^{t+t^{\prime}-1}\Psi_{i^{\prime},j^{\prime}}^{(s)}-O\left(\frac{t^{\prime}\Xi_{2}^{2}}{d^{3/2}}\right)

where the bounds for
\Phi_{i^{\prime},j^{\prime}}^{(s)}
for each
s\in[t_{1},t_{1}+t^{\prime}]
are obtained from induction over iterations
s^{\prime}\in\left[\frac{d^{1.01}}{\eta},s\right]
. Therefore there must exist
t^{\prime\prime\prime}\in[t,t+t^{\prime}]
such that
\left|\Psi_{i^{\prime},j^{\prime}}^{(t^{\prime\prime\prime})}\right|\leq O\left(\frac{\sqrt{\Xi_{2}}}{\tau\sqrt{d}t\eta}\right)
or otherwise
\mathfrak{F}_{j}^{(t+t^{\prime})}\geq\mathfrak{F}_{j}^{(t)}+t^{\prime}\cdot O\left(\frac{\sqrt{\Xi_{2}}\tau\log d}{\sqrt{d}t\eta}\right)\geq\Delta+\delta\tau\log d
, which results in that
\left|\Psi_{i^{\prime},j^{\prime}}^{(t)}\right|\leq O\left(\frac{\tau\log d}{\sqrt{d}t\eta}\right)\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}
, following the same reasoning in Lemma
E.3
. Above arguments actually proved that
\left|\Psi_{i^{\prime},j^{\prime}}^{(t)}\right|\leq\Omega\left(\frac{\Xi_{2}}{\sqrt{d}t\eta}\right)\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}
at all
t\in\left[\frac{d\,\operatorname{polylog}(d)}{\eta},\frac{d^{1.498}}{\eta}\right]
. Therefore we can use the results of all
\Psi_{i^{\prime},j^{\prime}}^{(t)}
, where
j^{\prime}\neq j,j^{\prime}\in[d]
to get (combined with Fact
E.1
):

|H_{1}|\leq\widetilde{O}\left(\max_{j^{\prime}\neq j,j^{\prime}\in[d]}\Psi_{i,j}^{(t)}\right)\leq\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\Xi_{2}^{2}}{d^{2}}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}\right)

(388)

For iterations
t\geq\frac{d^{1.498}}{\eta}
, the proof is essentially the same: we only need to notice that the difference
\Psi_{i,j}^{(t)}-\lambda\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle
here will bounce around zero, while the compensation terms in
H_{1}
are bounded by
\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{d^{1.98}}\right)
. These observations indeed prove the case
i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}^{\star}
. When
i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}\setminus\mathcal{M}_{j}^{\star}
, notice that
with prob
\leq\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{1}{d}\right)
it holds
\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},z^{(s)}_{Y}\rangle|\geq b_{i}^{(t)}}=\bm{1}_{\tilde{z}_{n,j^{\prime}}^{+(s)}\neq 0}
for any
x\in\mathfrak{B}
. Now we expand

\displaystyle H_{1}

(389)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j^{\prime}\in\mathcal{N}_{i},j^{\prime}\neq j}\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\mathbb{E}\left[\left((\ell_{p,t}^{\prime}-1)\cdot\sum_{s=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,j^{\prime}}^{+(s)}+\sum_{\bm{X}_{n,s}\in\mathcal{N}}\ell_{s,t}^{\prime}\cdot\sum_{q=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,s,j^{\prime}}^{(q)}\bm{1}_{\tilde{z}_{n,s,j^{\prime}}^{(q)}\neq 0}\right)\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{j^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{N}_{i},j^{\prime}\neq j}\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\bm{M}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\mathbb{E}\left[\left((\ell_{p,t}^{\prime}-1)\cdot\sum_{s=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,j^{\prime}}^{+(s)}+\sum_{\bm{X}_{n,s}\in\mathcal{N}}\ell_{s,t}^{\prime}\cdot\sum_{q=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,s,j^{\prime}}^{(q)}\bm{1}_{\tilde{z}_{n,s,j^{\prime}}^{(q)}\neq 0}\right)\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

Indeed, the event that there are some
j^{\prime}\in\mathcal{N}_{i}
(which means
i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}
) such that
z_{p,j^{\prime}}\neq 0

has probability
\leq\widetilde{O}(1/d)
. Thus the first term on the RHS is trivially bounded by
\widetilde{O}(1/d^{2})\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}
. For the second term of
H_{1}
, we can again go through similar procedure as above to obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[(\ell_{p,t}^{\prime}-1)\sum_{s=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,j^{\prime}}^{+(s)}+\sum_{\bm{X}_{n,s}\in\mathfrak{N}}\ell_{s,t}^{\prime}\sum_{q=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,s,j^{\prime}}^{(q)}\bm{1}_{\tilde{z}_{n,s,j^{\prime}}^{(q)}\neq 0}\right]\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}

(390)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max\left\{\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\Xi_{2}}}{\sqrt{d}t\eta}\right),\frac{1}{d^{1.99}}\right\}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}

Then again we have

|H_{1}|\leq\widetilde{O}\left(\max_{j^{\prime}\neq j,j^{\prime}\in[d]}\Psi_{i,j}^{(t)}\right)\leq\widetilde{O}\left(\max\left\{\frac{\Xi_{2}^{2}}{d^{2}},\frac{\Xi_{2}}{\sqrt{d}t\eta}\right\}\right)\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}

(391)

which can be combined with the bound for
H_{2}
to conclude the proof.
∎

J.6
Proof of Lemma
E.6
:

Proof of Lemma
E.6
:

By using Bernoulli concentration, we know that whenever
\sum_{j\in[d]}\mathbf{1}_{\sum_{r=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j}|\neq 0}=\Omega(\log\log d)
(which happens with constant probability), we have

\sum_{j\in[d]}\mathbf{1}_{\sum_{q=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}^{(q)}_{n,s,j}|=\sum_{r=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j}|}\leq C\sum_{j\in[d]}\mathbf{1}_{\sum_{r=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j}|\neq 0}.

(392)

\text{(with prob }\geq 1-\frac{1}{d^{\Omega(\log\log d)}}\text{ for all }X_{n,s}\in\mathfrak{N})

And also from Definition
E.2
we know that if for some
j\in[d]
,
\sum_{r=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j}|=\sum_{q=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}^{(q)}_{n,s,j}|
, then

\sum_{i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}^{\star}}\left(f_{t,\theta^{\star},i}(X_{n})h_{i,t}(X_{n,s})-f_{t,\theta^{\star},i}(X_{n})h_{i,t}(Y_{n})\right)\geq\kappa\tau\sum_{j\in\mathcal{M}_{j}^{\star}}|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\mathbf{M}_{j}\rangle|+O\left(\frac{1}{\log d}\right)

(393)

which can be obtained by similar calculations in Lemma
E.2
.

Noticing that the event
\sum_{r=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n}|\neq 0
happens with
\operatorname{prob}\geq 1-\frac{1}{\operatorname{polylog}(d)}
, we have

\displaystyle\widetilde{L}(f_{t,\theta^{\star}},f_{t})

(394)

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{1}{\operatorname{polylog}(d)}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\log\left(\sum_{X\in\mathfrak{B}}e^{\langle f_{t,\theta^{\star}}(X_{n}),f_{t}(X)\rangle/\tau-\langle f_{t,\theta^{\star}}(X_{n}),f_{t}(Y_{n})\rangle/\tau}\right)\,\middle|\,\sum_{r=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n}|\neq 0\right]

\displaystyle+\operatorname{Pr}(\sum_{r=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n}|=0)\cdot O(\log|\mathfrak{B}|)

\displaystyle\overset{(1)}{=}\left(1-\frac{1}{\operatorname{polylog}(d)}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\log\left(\sum_{X\in\mathfrak{B}}e^{\sum_{j\in[d]}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}^{\star}}(f_{t,\theta^{\star},i}(X_{n})h_{i,t}(X)-f_{t,\theta^{\star},i}(X_{n})h_{i,t}(Y_{n}))/\tau}\right)\,\middle|\,\sum_{r=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n}|\neq 0\right]

\displaystyle+\Pr(\sum_{r=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n}|=0)\cdot O(\log|\mathfrak{B}|)

\displaystyle\leq O\left(\frac{1}{\log d}\right)

(1) is because
\theta^{\star}
has a value only when
i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}^{\star}
occurs. where the last inequality combines the Bernoulli concentration results of
\sum_{j\in[d]}\sum_{r=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j}|\sum_{q=1}^{L}|\tilde{z}^{(q)}_{n,s,j}|
and a union bound for all
s\in[\mathfrak{N}]
, and that
\sum_{i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}^{\star}}|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\mathbf{M}_{j}\rangle|\geq\Omega\left(\frac{\sqrt{\tau}}{\Xi_{2}}\right)
.
∎

J.7
Proof of Lemma
E.7
(a):

Proof of Lemma
E.7
(a):

Since the mean of
\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle
is zero, we can simply compute the variance as

\displaystyle\operatorname{Var}\left(\langle\bm{w}_{i},z^{(r)\setminus j}_{X}\rangle+\frac{1}{L}\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j}\right)

(395)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2}{L^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\langle\bm{w}_{i},\sum_{j^{\prime}\neq j,j^{\prime}\in[d]}\bm{M}_{j^{\prime}}\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j^{\prime}}+\tilde{\xi}^{(r)}_{n}\rangle\right)^{2}\right]+\frac{2}{L^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4}{L^{2}}\sum_{s=1}^{d_{1}}(\bm{w}_{i})_{s}^{2}(M_{j^{\prime}})_{s}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{j^{\prime}\neq j}(\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j^{\prime}})^{2}\right]+\frac{4}{L^{2}}\sum_{s=1}^{d_{1}}(\bm{w}_{i})_{s}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[(\tilde{\xi}^{(r)}_{n,s})^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{2}{L^{2}}\sum_{s=1}^{d_{1}}(\bm{w}_{i})_{s}^{2}(\bm{M}_{j})_{s}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[(\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j^{\prime}})^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}}{d_{1}}\right)+\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}}{d}\right)+\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}}{d_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}}{d}\right).

Now we can use Chebychev’s inequality to conclude: For a random variable
X
with mean zero, Chebyshev’s inequality tells us:

\displaystyle\Pr(|X|\geq t)\leq\frac{\operatorname{Var}(X)}{t^{2}}

(396)

\displaystyle\Pr(|\langle\bm{w}_{i},z^{(r)\setminus j}_{X}\rangle+\frac{1}{L}\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j}|\geq t)\leq\frac{\operatorname{Var}(\langle\bm{w}_{i},z^{(r)\setminus j}_{X}\rangle+\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j})}{t^{2}}

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\left(\langle\bm{w}_{i},z^{(r)\setminus j}_{X}\rangle+\frac{1}{L}\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j}\right)^{2}\geq t^{2}\right)\leq\frac{\operatorname{Var}(\langle\bm{w}_{i},z^{(r)\setminus j}_{X}\rangle+\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j})}{t^{2}}

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\left(\langle\bm{w}_{i},z^{(r)\setminus j}_{X}\rangle+\frac{1}{L}\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j}\right)^{2}\geq\frac{\lambda\|\bm{w}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}\sqrt{\log d}}{d}\right)\leq\frac{\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}}{d}\right)}{\frac{\lambda\|\bm{w}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}\sqrt{\log d}}{d}}

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\left(\langle\bm{w}_{i},z^{(r)\setminus j}_{X}\rangle+\frac{1}{L}\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j}\right)^{2}\geq\frac{\lambda\|\bm{w}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}\sqrt{\log d}}{d}\right)\leq O\left(\frac{1}{\lambda}\right).

As to the tail bounds for other variables, it suffices to go through some similar calculations.
∎

J.8
Proof of Lemma
E.7
(b):

Proof of Lemma
E.7
(b):

\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{M}\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n}\rangle=\langle\bm{w}_{i},\sum^{d}_{j=1}\bm{M}_{j}\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j}\rangle=\sum^{d_{1}}_{s}(\bm{w}_{i})_{s}(\sum^{d}_{j=1}\bm{M}_{j}\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j})_{s}=\sum^{d_{1}}_{s}(\bm{w}_{i})_{s}(\sum^{d}_{j=1}M_{j,s}\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j}).

(397)

z^{(i)}_{n,j}
is a bounded random variable in the interval
[-1,1]
, so
z^{(i)}_{n,j}
is sub-Gaussian variable with variance proxy 1, then
\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j}=\sum_{i=1}^{L}\delta_{n,i}^{(r)}z^{(i)}_{n,j}
is also is sub-Gaussian variable with variance proxy
\Delta^{(r)}_{n}
.
\sum^{d}_{j=1}M_{j,s}\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n,j}
is sub-Gaussian variable with variance proxy
\max_{j\in[d]}\|\bm{M}_{j}\|_{\infty}^{2}
, so
\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{M}\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n}\rangle
is sub-Gaussian variable with variance proxy
\|\bm{w}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}\cdot\max_{j\in[d]}\|\bm{M}_{j}\|_{\infty}^{2}
.

Sub-Gaussian Tail Bound

\displaystyle\Pr[|X-\mu|\geq t]\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{t^{2}}{2\sigma^{2}}\right)

(398)

\displaystyle\Pr(\left|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{M}\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n}\rangle\right|\geq t)\leq 2\exp\left(\frac{-t^{2}}{\|\bm{w}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}\cdot\max_{j\in[d]}\|\bm{M}_{j}\|_{\infty}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\Pr(\left(\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{M}\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n}\rangle\right)^{2}\geq t^{2})\leq 2\exp\left(\frac{-t^{2}}{\|\bm{w}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}\cdot\max_{j\in[d]}\|\bm{M}_{j}\|_{\infty}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\left(\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{M}\tilde{z}^{(r)}_{n}\rangle\right)^{2}\geq\|\bm{w}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}\cdot\max_{j\in[d]}\|\bm{M}_{j}\|_{\infty}^{2}\log^{4}d\right)\lesssim e^{-\Omega(\log^{2}d)}.

∎

J.9
Proof of Lemma
E.7
(c):

Proof of Lemma
E.7
(c):

\xi^{(i)}_{n}
(
\xi^{(i)}_{n,k}
) is a Gaussian random vector (variable), so
\sum_{i=1}^{L}\delta_{n,i}^{(r)}\xi_{n}^{(i)}
(
\sum_{i=1}^{L}\delta_{n,i}^{(r)}\xi_{n,k}^{(i)}
) is a Gaussian random vector (variable), and therefore
\tilde{\xi}^{(r)}_{n}
(
\tilde{\xi}^{(r)}_{n,k}
) is a Gaussian random vector (variable).
\tilde{\xi}^{(r)}_{n,k}
is a sub-Gaussian random variable and each term
w_{i,k}\cdot\tilde{\xi}^{(r)}_{n,k}
is a sub-Gaussian random variable, and its variance is:
\Delta^{(r)}_{n}\cdot w_{i,k}^{2}\cdot\sigma_{\xi}^{2}
(
\Delta^{(r)}_{n}=\sum_{i=1}^{L}(\delta_{n,i}^{(r)})^{2}
). Therefore
\sum_{k=1}^{d_{1}}w_{i,k}\cdot\tilde{\xi}^{(r)}_{n,k}
is a sub-Gaussian random variable, and its variance is:
\sum_{k=1}^{d_{1}}\Delta^{(r)}_{n}\cdot w_{i,k}^{2}\cdot\sigma_{\xi}^{2}=\Delta^{(r)}_{n}\cdot\|\bm{w}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}\cdot\sigma_{\xi}^{2}
.

Sub-Gaussian Tail Bound

\displaystyle\Pr[|X-\mu|\geq t]\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{t^{2}}{2\sigma^{2}}\right)

(399)

\displaystyle\Pr(\left|\sum_{k=1}^{d_{1}}w_{i,k}\cdot\tilde{\xi}^{(r)}_{n,k}\right|\geq t)\leq 2\exp\left(\frac{-t^{2}}{2\Delta^{(r)}_{n}\|\bm{w}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{\xi}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\Pr(\left(\sum_{k=1}^{d_{1}}w_{i,k}\cdot\tilde{\xi}^{(r)}_{n,k}\right)^{2}\geq t^{2})\leq 2\exp\left(\frac{-t^{2}}{2\Delta^{(r)}_{n}\|\bm{w}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}\sigma_{\xi}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\left(\sum_{k=1}^{d_{1}}w_{i,k}\cdot\tilde{\xi}^{(r)}_{n,k}\right)^{2}\geq\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}\log^{4}d}{d}\right)\lesssim e^{-\Omega(\log^{2}d)}.

∎

Proof of Lemma
E.8
:

The proof is similar to the proof of
(
Allen-Zhu & Li 2022
;
Wen & Li 2021
)
.
∎

Proof of Lemma
E.9
:

The proof is similar to the proof of
(
Allen-Zhu & Li 2022
;
Wen & Li 2021
)
.
∎

J.10
Proof of Lemma
E.10
(a):

Proof of Lemma
E.10
(a):

In the proof we will make the following simplification of notations: we drop the time superscript
(t)
, and also the subscript for neuron index
i
. We start with the case when
0<\alpha_{j}<b_{i}^{(t)}
and rewrite the expectation as follows:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[h_{i}(\bm{Y}_{n})\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

(400)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\left(\text{ReLU}\left(\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle-b_{i}\right)-\text{ReLU}\left(-\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle-b_{i}\right)\right)\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\left(\left(\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle-b_{i}\right)\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}-\left(-\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle-b_{i}\right)\bm{1}_{-\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\right)\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\left(\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle-b_{i}\right)\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right.

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\left.\sum_{s=1}^{L}\left(-\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle-b_{i}\right)\bm{1}_{-\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}-b_{i}\sum_{s=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right.

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\left.\sum_{s=1}^{L}\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\bm{1}_{-\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+b_{i}\sum_{s=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{-\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[b_{i}\sum_{s=1}^{L}\left(\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}-\bm{1}_{-\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\right)\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

Thus the expectation can be expanded as:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[h_{i}(\bm{Y}_{n})\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

(401)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\langle\bm{w}_{i},z_{Y}^{(s)}\rangle\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[b_{i}\sum_{s=1}^{L}\left(\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}-\bm{1}_{-\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\right)\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{L}\mathbb{E}\left[\alpha_{j}\sum_{s=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle|\geq b_{i}+|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}-\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle|}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\left(S^{(s)\setminus j}-b_{i}\right)\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\left(S^{(s)\setminus j}+b_{i}\right)\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\leq-b_{i}}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle\leq-b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\left(\frac{1}{L}\alpha_{j}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}+S^{(s)\setminus j}-b_{i}\right)\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle\leq-b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\left(\frac{1}{L}\alpha_{j}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}+S^{(s)\setminus j}+b_{i}\right)\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\leq-b_{i}}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}

Now we need to obtain absolute bounds for both
J_{2}
and
J_{3}
. We start with
J_{2}
, where

\displaystyle J_{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\left(S^{(s)\setminus j}-b_{i}\right)\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

(402)

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\left(S^{(s)\setminus j}+b_{i}\right)\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\leq-b_{i}}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle\leq-b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\left(S^{(s)\setminus j}-b_{i}\right)\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle\geq b_{i}+|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}-\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle|}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\left(S^{(s)\setminus j}+b_{i}\right)\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle\leq-b_{i}-|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}-\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle|}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

We proceed with the first term

\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\left(S^{(s)\setminus j}-b_{i}\right)\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle\geq b_{i}+|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}-\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle|}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

(403)

First, from a trivial calculation conditioned on the randomness of
\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}
, we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\left(S^{(s)\setminus j}-b_{i}\right)\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle\geq b_{i}+|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}-\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle|}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

(404)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\left(S^{(s)\setminus j}-b_{i}\right)\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle\geq b_{i}+|\bar{\mathcal{S}}^{(r,s)\setminus j}+\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}|}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\left(S^{(s)\setminus j}-b_{i}\right)\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{\mathcal{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}\geq b_{i}-\alpha_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}+|\bar{\mathcal{S}}^{(r,s)\setminus j}+\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}|}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\bigl(S^{(s)\setminus j}-b_{i}\bigr)\sum_{r=1}^{L}\lvert\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\rvert\Big(\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}\geq b_{i}-\alpha_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}+\bigl\lvert\bar{\mathcal{S}}^{(r,s)\setminus j}+\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\tfrac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\bigr\rvert}

\displaystyle-\,\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}\geq b_{i}+\alpha_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}+\bigl\lvert\bar{\mathcal{S}}^{(r,s)\setminus j}-\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\tfrac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\bigr\rvert}\Big)\Bigg].

Now define:

\displaystyle Z

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left(\left|\bar{\mathcal{S}}^{(r,s)\setminus j}+\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\right|+\left|\bar{\mathcal{S}}^{(r,s)\setminus j}-\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\right|\right),

(405)

\displaystyle Z^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left(\left|\bar{\mathcal{S}}^{(r,s)\setminus j}+\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\right|-\left|\bar{\mathcal{S}}^{(r,s)\setminus j}-\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\right|\right).

In this case, we always have
|Z^{\prime}|\leq|\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}||\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}|
, and

\displaystyle\Biggl\lvert\bm{1}_{\mathcal{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}\geq b_{i}-\alpha_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}+\bigl\lvert\bar{\mathcal{S}}^{(r,s)\setminus j}+\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\tfrac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\bigr\rvert}

(406)

\displaystyle-\bm{1}_{\mathcal{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}\geq b_{i}+\alpha_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}+\bigl\lvert\bar{\mathcal{S}}^{(r,s)\setminus j}-\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\tfrac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\bigr\rvert}\Biggr\rvert

\displaystyle=\bm{1}_{\mathcal{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}-b_{i}-Z\in[-|\alpha_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}-Z^{\prime}|,|\alpha_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}-Z^{\prime}|]}

Equality Proof: The original two threshold values are:

\displaystyle T_{1}=b_{i}-\alpha_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}+\left|\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}+\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\right|

(407)

\displaystyle T_{2}=b_{i}+\alpha_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}+\left|\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}-\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\right|

We can rewrite these two terms as:

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle=b_{i}+\left(-\alpha_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}+\left|\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}+\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\right|\right)

(408)

\displaystyle T_{2}

\displaystyle=b_{i}+\left(\alpha_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}+\left|\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}-\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\right|\right)

Then the midpoint between these two terms is:

\text{Midpoint}=b_{i}+Z

(409)

The distance between them is:

\displaystyle T_{2}-T_{1}

(410)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\alpha_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}+\left(\left|\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}-\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\right|-\left|\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}+\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\right|\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2(\alpha_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}-Z^{\prime})

Therefore, shifting from the midpoint
b_{i}+Z
by
\left|\alpha_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}-Z^{\prime}\right|
in both directions will exactly span the distance between
T_{1}
and
T_{2}
.

The indicator function differs by 1 if and only if:

T_{1}\leq S^{(r,s)\setminus j}<T_{2}

(411)

Therefore we have:

\displaystyle\Biggl\lvert\bm{1}_{\mathcal{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}\geq b_{i}-\alpha_{j}\tfrac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}+\bigl\lvert\bar{\mathcal{S}}^{(r,s)\setminus j}+\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\tfrac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\bigr\rvert}

(412)

\displaystyle-\bm{1}_{\mathcal{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}\geq b_{i}+\alpha_{j}\tfrac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}+\bigl\lvert\bar{\mathcal{S}}^{(r,s)\setminus j}-\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\tfrac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\bigr\rvert}\Biggr\rvert

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\bm{1}_{S^{(r,s)\setminus j}\geq T_{1}}-\bm{1}_{S^{(r,s)\setminus j}\geq T_{2}}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bm{1}_{S^{(r,s)\setminus j}-\frac{T_{1}+T_{2}}{2}\in[-\frac{T_{2}-T_{1}}{2},\frac{T_{2}-T_{1}}{2}]}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bm{1}_{S^{(r,s)\setminus j}-b_{i}-Z\in[-|\alpha_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}-Z^{\prime}|,\ |\alpha_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}-Z^{\prime}|]}

Inequality Proof: Let
a=\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}
,
b=\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}
.

We know:
\left|\left|a+b\right|-\left|a-b\right|\right|\leq 2|b|
and
\left|\left|a+b\right|+\left|a-b\right|\right|\leq 2(|a|+|b|)

Applying this, we have:

\displaystyle|Z^{\prime}|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left|\,|a+b|-|a-b|\,\right|\leq\frac{1}{2}\cdot 2|b|=|b|=|\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}|

(413)

\displaystyle|Z|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left|\,|a+b|+|a-b|\,\right|\leq\frac{1}{2}\cdot 2\cdot(|a|+|b|)=|\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}|+|\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}|

Which allows us to proceed as follows:

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\left(S^{(s)\setminus j}-b_{i}\right)\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle\geq b_{i}+|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}-\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle|}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]\right|

(414)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Biggl\lvert\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\bigl(S^{(s)\setminus j}-b_{i}-Z+Z\bigr)\sum_{r=1}^{L}\lvert\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\rvert\Big(\bm{1}_{\mathcal{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}\geq b_{i}-\alpha_{j}\tfrac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}+\bigl\lvert\bar{\mathcal{S}}^{(r,s)\setminus j}+\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\tfrac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\bigr\rvert}

\displaystyle-\bm{1}_{\mathcal{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}\geq b_{i}+\alpha_{j}\tfrac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}+\bigl\lvert\bar{\mathcal{S}}^{(r,s)\setminus j}-\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\tfrac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\bigr\rvert}\Big)\,\bm{1}_{S^{\setminus j}\geq b_{i}-\alpha_{j}\tfrac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}}\Bigg]\Biggr\rvert

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{L}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}(\alpha_{j}+2|\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}|)|\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2}||\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}|\bm{1}_{S^{(r,s)\setminus j}\geq b_{i}-\alpha_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}|\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}||\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}|\bm{1}_{S^{(r,s)\setminus j}-b_{i}-Z\in[-|\alpha_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}-Z^{\prime}|,|\alpha_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}-Z^{\prime}|]}\bm{1}_{S^{(r,s)\setminus j}\geq b_{i}-\alpha_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{L}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}(\alpha_{j}+2|\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}|)|\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2}||\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}|\bm{1}_{S^{(r,s)\setminus j}\geq b_{i}-\alpha_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[|\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}|^{2}(\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)})^{2}\bm{1}_{S^{(r,s)\setminus j}\geq b_{i}-\alpha_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}}\right]}\cdot\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{1}_{S^{(r,s)\setminus j}-b_{i}-Z\in[-|\alpha_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}-Z^{\prime}|,|\alpha_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}-Z^{\prime}|]}\right]}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{\textcircled{\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{1}}}}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{L}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}(\alpha_{j}+2|\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}|)|\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2}||\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}|\bm{1}_{S^{(r,s)\setminus j}\geq b_{i}-\alpha_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[|\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}|^{2}(\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)})^{2}\bm{1}_{S^{(r,s)\setminus j}\geq b_{i}-\alpha_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}}\right]}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[(\alpha_{j}+|\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}|)^{2}(\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L})^{2}\right]}{\mathbb{E}\left[\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)}+\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{+(s)}}{2L}\rangle^{2}\right]}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(1)\left(\alpha_{j}+O\left(\mathbb{E}[|\bar{\alpha}_{j}|^{2}]^{1/2}\right)\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}|\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2}||\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}|\right]\left(\Pr(S^{\setminus j}\geq b_{i}-\alpha_{j}C_{\tilde{z}})+\sqrt{\frac{\mathbb{E}[|\bar{S}^{\setminus j}|^{2}\bm{1}_{S^{\setminus j}\geq b_{i}-\alpha_{j}C_{\tilde{z}}}]}{\mathbb{E}[\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{\tilde{\xi}_{n}+\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{+}}{2L}\rangle^{2}]}}\right)

where in
\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{1}}⃝
we have used the randomness of
\frac{\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)}+\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{+(s)}}{2L}
in the following manner: Fixing the randomness of
\tilde{z}_{n,j}
, we have
S^{(r,s)\setminus j}-Z
is a random variable depending solely on the randomness of
\frac{\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)}+\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{+(s)}}{2L}
, and thus we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{1}_{S^{(r,s)\setminus j}-b_{i}-Z\in[-|\alpha_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}-Z^{\prime}|,|\alpha_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}-Z^{\prime}|]}\right]

(415)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)}+\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{+(s)}}{2L}\rangle-|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{+(s)}\rangle|\in[|\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}|-(\alpha_{j}+|\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}|),\alpha_{j}+2|\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}|]}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)}}{2L}\rangle+\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{+(s)}}{2L}\rangle-|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{+(s)}}{L}\rangle|\in[-O(\alpha_{j}+|\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}|),O(\alpha_{j}+|\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}|)]}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{O((\alpha_{j}+|\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}|)^{2}(\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L})^{2})}{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)}+\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{+(s)}}{2L}\rangle^{2}}\right]=\frac{\mathbb{E}[O((\alpha_{j}+|\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}|)^{2}(\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L})^{2}))]}{\mathbb{E}[\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)}+\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{+(s)}}{2L}\rangle^{2}]}

Simultaneously, from similar analysis as above, we have for the second term in
J_{2}
:

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\left(S^{(s)\setminus j}+b_{i}\right)\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle\leq-b_{i}-|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}-\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle|}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]\right|

(416)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\left(S^{(s)\setminus j}+b_{i}\right)\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle\leq-b_{i}-|\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}+\bar{\alpha}_{j}\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}|}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O\left(\alpha_{j}+\mathbb{E}[|\bar{\alpha}_{j}|^{2}]^{1/2}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}|\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2}||\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}|\right]\sqrt{\frac{\mathbb{E}[|\bar{S}^{\setminus j}|^{2}\bm{1}_{S^{\setminus j}\geq b_{i}-\alpha_{j}}]}{\mathbb{E}[\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{\tilde{\xi}_{n}+\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{+}}{2L}\rangle^{2}]}}

Now we turn to
J_{3}

\displaystyle J_{3}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\left(\frac{1}{L}\alpha_{j}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}+S^{(s)\setminus j}-b_{i}\right)\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle\leq-b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

(417)

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\left(\frac{1}{L}\alpha_{j}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}+S^{(s)\setminus j}+b_{i}\right)\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\leq-b_{i}}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\text{\textcircled{\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{1}}}}}{=}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\left(\frac{1}{L}\alpha_{j}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}+S^{(s)\setminus j}\right)\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle\leq-b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\left(\frac{1}{L}\alpha_{j}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}+S^{(s)\setminus j}\right)\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\leq-b_{i}}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{1}}⃝
is from the symmetry of
\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}
and
\sum_{s=1}^{L}\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}
over the randomness of
z_{X},z_{Y}
, (
\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\overset{d}{=}\sum_{s=1}^{L}\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}
) we observe

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}f(\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)})

\displaystyle\overset{d}{=}\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}f(\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)})

(418)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}f(\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)})]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}f(\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)})]

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}b_{i}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\leq-b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}b_{i}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle\leq-b_{i}}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

which allows us to drop the
b_{i}
terms in
J_{3}
. The analysis of the rest of
J_{3}
is somewhat similar.

First we observe that whenever
\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle\leq-b_{i}}\neq 0
. we have
\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\geq b_{i}\quad\text{and}\quad\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle\leq-b_{i}
, so we can get
\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}+\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\geq b_{i}+|S^{(r,s)\setminus j}+\alpha_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}|

When this inequality holds, we always have
\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}\geq b_{i}-\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}
.

Together with all the above observations, we proceed to compute as:

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\left(\frac{1}{L}\alpha_{j}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}+S^{(s)\setminus j}\right)\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle\leq-b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]\right|

(419)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\left(\frac{1}{L}\alpha_{j}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}+S^{(s)\setminus j}\right)\bm{1}_{\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}+\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}\geq b_{i}+|\alpha_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}+S^{(r,s)\setminus j}|}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\biggl\lvert\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}+\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\tfrac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\biggr\rvert\,\lvert\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\rvert\,\bm{1}_{\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}\geq b_{i}-\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\tfrac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}}

\displaystyle\cdot\;\bm{1}_{\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}\in\bigl[\,b_{i}-\alpha_{j}\bigl\lvert\tfrac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\bigr\rvert+\bigl\lvert\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}+\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\tfrac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\bigr\rvert,\;b_{i}+\alpha_{j}\bigl\lvert\tfrac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\bigr\rvert+\bigl\lvert\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}-\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\tfrac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\bigr\rvert\,\bigr]}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\!\left[\lvert\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}\rvert^{2}\lvert\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\rvert^{2}\right]\bm{1}_{\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}\geq b_{i}-\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\tfrac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}}}

\displaystyle\cdot\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\!\left[\bm{1}_{\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}\in\bigl[\,b_{i}-\alpha_{j}\bigl\lvert\tfrac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\bigr\rvert+\bigl\lvert\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}+\alpha_{j}\tfrac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\bigr\rvert,\;b_{i}+\alpha_{j}\bigl\lvert\tfrac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\bigr\rvert+\bigl\lvert\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}-\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\tfrac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\bigr\rvert\,\bigr]}\right]}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{L}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}|\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2}||\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}|\bm{1}_{\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}\geq b_{i}-\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\!\left[\lvert\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}\rvert^{2}\lvert\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\rvert^{2}\right]\bm{1}_{\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}\geq b_{i}-\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\tfrac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}}}

\displaystyle\cdot\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\!\left[\bm{1}_{\left\langle\bm{w}_{i},\tfrac{\tilde{\xi}_{n,j}^{(r)}-\tilde{\xi}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\right\rangle\in\bigl[-\,\bigl\lvert\alpha_{j}\tfrac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}+\lvert\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\rvert\tfrac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\bigr\rvert,\;\bigl\lvert\alpha_{j}\tfrac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}+\lvert\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\rvert\tfrac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\bigr\rvert\bigr]}\right]}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{L}O(\alpha_{j}+\mathbb{E}[|\bar{\alpha}_{j}|^{2}]^{1/2})\mathbb{E}[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}|\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2}||\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}|]\cdot\sqrt{\frac{\mathbb{E}[|\bar{S}^{\setminus j}|^{2}\bm{1}_{\bar{S}^{\setminus j}\geq b_{i}-\bar{\alpha}_{j}C_{\tilde{z}}}]}{\mathbb{E}[\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)}+\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{+(s)}}{2L}\rangle^{2}]}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{L}\left(\mathbb{E}[|\bar{\alpha}_{j}|^{2}]^{1/2}\mathbb{E}[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}|\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2}||\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}|]\right)\Pr(\bar{S}^{\setminus j}\geq b_{i}-\bar{\alpha}_{j}C_{\tilde{z}})

where in the last inequality, we have use the following reasoning: we know that
\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{+(s)}-\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)}}{2L}\rangle
has the same distribution with
\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)}+\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{+(s)}}{2L}\rangle
.(
\langle\bm{w}_{i},(\frac{\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{+(s)}-\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)}}{2L}\rangle\overset{d}{=}\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)}+\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{+(s)}}{2L}\rangle
) We use the randomness to obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)},\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{+(s)}}

\displaystyle\left[\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)}-\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{+(s)}}{2L}\rangle\in[-|\alpha_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}+|\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}|\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}|,|\alpha_{j}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}+|\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}|\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}|]}\right]

(420)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{|\alpha_{j}+\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}|^{2}\cdot|\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}|^{2}}{\mathbb{E}_{\xi}[\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{+(s)}-\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)}}{2L}\rangle^{2}]}=\frac{|\alpha_{j}+\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}|^{2}\cdot|\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}|^{2}}{\mathbb{E}_{\xi}[\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)}+\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{+(s)}}{2L}\rangle^{2}]}

The second term of
J_{3}
can be similarly bounded by the same quantity. Now by combining all the results of
J_{1},J_{2},J_{3}
above, we have the desired result for (a).

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}

\displaystyle\left[h_{i}(\bm{Y}_{n})\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

(421)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{L}\alpha_{i,j}^{(t)}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},\frac{z_{X}+z_{Y}}{2}\rangle|\geq b_{i}+|\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)},z_{X}-\frac{z_{X}+z_{Y}}{2}\rangle|}\right]

\displaystyle\pm

\displaystyle\left(\alpha_{i,j}^{(t)}+O\left(\sqrt{\mathbb{E}|\bar{\alpha}_{i,j}^{(t)}|^{2}}\right)\right)\cdot\mathbb{E}[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}|\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2}||\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}|]\cdot O(L_{1}+L_{2})

∎

J.11
Proof of Lemma
E.10
(b):

Proof of Lemma
E.10
(b):

This proof is extremely similar to the above proof of Lemma
E.10
(a), we describe the differences here and sketch the remaining. First we need to decompose the expectation as follows:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[h_{i}(\bm{Y}_{n})\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{L}\delta_{n,i}^{(r)}z_{n,j}^{(i)}\right)\right]

(422)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{L}(\alpha_{j}-b_{i})\sum_{s=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle|\geq b_{i}+|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}-\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle|}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\left(S^{(s)\setminus j}\right)\sum_{r=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle|\geq b_{i}+|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}-\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle|}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\left(\frac{1}{L}\alpha_{j}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}+S^{(s)\setminus j}\right)\sum_{r=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},_{X}^{(r)}-\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle|\geq b_{i}+|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle|}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}

where we have used the following facts:

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}[b_{i}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},z_{X}^{(r)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},z_{Y}^{(s)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}]=-\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}[b_{i}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},z_{X}^{(r)}\rangle\leq-b_{i}}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},z_{Y}^{(s)}\rangle\leq-b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}];

(423)

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}[b_{i}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},z_{X}^{(r)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},z_{Y}^{(s)}\rangle\leq-b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}]=\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\mathbb{E}[b_{i}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},z_{Y}^{(s)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},z_{X}^{(r)}\rangle\leq-b_{i}}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}];

\displaystyle\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},z_{X}^{(r)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},z_{Y}^{(s)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}=\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle\geq b_{i}+|\langle\bm{w}_{i},z_{X}^{(r)}-\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle|};

\displaystyle\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},z_{X}^{(r)}\rangle\leq-b_{i}}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},z_{Y}^{(s)}\rangle\leq-b_{i}}=\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle\leq-b_{i}-|\langle\bm{w}_{i},z_{X}^{(r)}-\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle|};

\displaystyle\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},z_{X}^{(r)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},z_{Y}^{(s)}\rangle\leq-b_{i}}=\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},z_{X}^{(r)}\rangle\leq-b_{i}}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},z_{Y}^{(s)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}=\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},z_{X}^{(r)}-\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle|\geq b_{i}+|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle|}.

Now observe that
J_{2}
can be dealt with as follows: define events
A_{3}:=\{|\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}+\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}C_{\tilde{z}}|\geq(\alpha_{j}C_{\tilde{z}}-b_{i})/2\}
and
A_{4}:=\{\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}\geq(\alpha_{j}C_{\tilde{z}}-b_{i})/2\}
, and notice that
\bm{1}\leq\bm{1}_{A_{3}}+\bm{1}_{A_{4}}
, we can compute

\displaystyle J_{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\left(S^{(s)\setminus j}\right)\sum_{r=1}^{L}\mathbf{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle|\geq b_{i}+|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}-\frac{z_{X}^{(r)}+z_{Y}^{(s)}}{2}\rangle|}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]

(424)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\bigl(S^{(s)\setminus j}\bigr)\sum_{r=1}^{L}\lvert\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\rvert\left(\mathbf{1}_{A_{3}}+\mathbf{1}_{A_{4}}\mathbf{1}_{A_{3}^{c}}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\mathbf{1}_{\,S^{(r,s)\setminus j}\in\bigl[\,b_{i}-\alpha_{j}\bigl\lvert\tfrac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\bigr\rvert+\bigl\lvert\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}+\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\tfrac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\bigr\rvert,\;b_{i}+\alpha_{j}\bigl\lvert\tfrac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\bigr\rvert+\bigl\lvert\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}-\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}\tfrac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\bigr\rvert\,\bigr]}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\overset{(1)}{\leq}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\left(O(\alpha_{j}+|\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}|)|\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}||\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}|\mathbf{1}_{A_{3}}+|\left(S^{(s)\setminus j}\right)||\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}|(\mathbf{1}_{A_{3}}+\mathbf{1}_{A_{4}})\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\mathbf{1}_{S^{(r,s)\setminus j}\in[\alpha_{j}|\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}|-b_{i}+|\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}+\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}|\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}||,b_{i}+\alpha_{j}|\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}|+|\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}-\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}|\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}||]}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{L}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}\right]\cdot O(\alpha_{j}+\mathbb{E}[|\bar{\alpha}_{j}|^{2}]^{1/2})\cdot\left(\mathrm{Pr}(A_{3})+\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[|\bar{S}^{\setminus j}|^{2}(\mathbf{1}_{A_{3}}+\mathbf{1}_{A_{4}})]/\mathbb{E}[\langle\bm{w}_{i},\xi_{p}\rangle^{2}]}\right)

where
\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{1}}⃝
relies on the fact that whenever
\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\rangle\geq b_{i}+|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}}{L}-\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}\rangle|}\neq 0
, we have
|S^{(s)\setminus j}|\leq b_{i}+(\alpha_{j}+|\bar{\alpha}^{(r,s)}_{j}|)|\frac{\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{(r)}+\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}}{2L}|+|\bar{S}^{(r,s)\setminus j}|
, and also that we have assumed
b_{i}<\alpha_{j}C_{\tilde{z}}
. The term
J_{3}
can be bounded from similar analysis as in the proof of Lemma
E.10
(a), but changing the factor from
O(L_{1}+L_{2})
to
O(L_{3}+L_{4})
. Combining these calculations gives the desired results.
∎

J.12
Proof of Lemma
E.11
:

Proof of Lemma
E.11
:

Again in this proof we omit the time superscript
(t)
. First we deal with the case where the features under consideration is
\bm{M}_{j},j\in[d]
.
\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)}
and
\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{+(r)}
are independent. Now we can write as follows:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[h_{i}(\bm{Y}_{n})\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\langle\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\right]

(425)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\left(\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle-b_{i}\right)\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\langle\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\left(\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle+b_{i}\right)\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\leq-b_{i}}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\langle\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle I_{1}+I_{2}

For the first term on the RHS, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\left(\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle-b_{i}\right)\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\langle\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\right]

(426)

where
\{\bm{M}_{j}\}_{j\in[d]}
is a basis for
\mathbb{R}^{d_{1}}
satisfying
\|\bm{M}_{j}\|_{\infty}\leq O(1/\sqrt{d_{1}})
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\left(\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle-b_{i}\right)\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\langle\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\right]

(427)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\bigl(\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle-b_{i}\bigr)\mathbf{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\Bigl(\mathbf{1}_{\lvert\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle+2(\alpha-\alpha^{\prime})\lvert\langle\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\rvert\rvert\geq b_{i}}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{\lvert\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle-2(\alpha-\alpha^{\prime})\lvert\langle\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\rvert\rvert\geq b_{i}}\Bigr)\,\lvert\langle\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\rvert\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(\bm{I}-\bm{M}_{j}\bm{M}_{j}^{\top})\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)}}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\left(\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle-b_{i}\right)\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\left|\langle\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\right|\right]

\displaystyle\times

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(\bm{I}-\bm{M}_{j}\bm{M}_{j}^{\top})\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)}}\left[\left|\bm{1}_{\left|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle+2(\alpha-\alpha^{\prime})|\langle\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|\right|\geq b_{i}}-\bm{1}_{\left|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle-2(\alpha-\alpha^{\prime})|\langle\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle|\right|\geq b_{i}}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O(1)\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[(\alpha_{j}+|\bar{\alpha}_{j}|)^{2}\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\langle\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle^{3}\cdot\frac{\left[\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle-b_{i}\right]}{\mathbb{E}[\langle\bm{w}_{i},\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)}\rangle^{2}]}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\right]

where in the last inequality we have used the randomness of
(\bm{I}-\bm{M}_{j}\bm{M}_{j}^{\top})\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)}
, which allow us to obtain the denominator
\Omega(1)\mathbb{E}|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)}\rangle|^{2}
.

Noticing that
\sum_{j\in[d]}(|\alpha_{j}|^{2}+|\bar{\alpha}_{j}|^{2})=\mathcal{O}(\|\bm{w}_{i}\|_{2}^{2})
coupled with Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, we can similarly obtain:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{s=1}^{L}\left(\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle-b_{i}\right)\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\bm{1}_{|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{X}}^{(r)}\rangle|\geq b_{i}}\langle\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\right]

(428)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O(1)\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[(\alpha_{j}+|\bar{\alpha}_{j}|)^{2}\cdot\sum_{s=1}^{L}\sum_{r=1}^{L}\langle\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle^{3}\cdot\frac{\left[\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle-b_{i}\right]}{\mathbb{E}[\langle\bm{w}_{i},\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)}\rangle^{2}]}\bm{1}_{\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle\geq b_{i}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O(1)\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[(\alpha_{j}+|\bar{\alpha}_{j}|)^{2}\cdot\sum_{r=1}^{L}\langle\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{(r)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle^{3}\right]\cdot\frac{1}{\|\bm{w}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}/d}\times\|\bm{w}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}\cdot\Pr(h_{i,t}(\bm{Y}_{n})\neq 0)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}\|_{2}}{d^{2}}\right)\cdot\Pr(h_{i,t}(\bm{Y}_{n})\neq 0)

where in the second inequality we have used the following arguments: first we can compute

\displaystyle(\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{z_{Y}}^{(s)}\rangle-b_{i})

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j\in\mathcal{N}_{i}}|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}|+\left|\sum_{j\notin\mathcal{N}_{i}}\langle\bm{w}_{i},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\tilde{z}_{n,j}^{+(s)}\right|+|\langle\bm{w}_{i},\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{+(s)}\rangle|

(429)

\displaystyle\leq\clubsuit+\spadesuit+\heartsuit

And from Lemma
D.1
, Lemma
E.7
. and Lemma
E.8
, we have

|\clubsuit|\geq\Omega(\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2})\quad\text{with prob}\leq\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{1}{d}\right)

(430)

|\spadesuit|,|\heartsuit|\leq\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{d}}\right)\quad\text{w.h.p.}

(431)

For the second term on the RHS, we can prove it in a similar way. Summing up over
I_{1}
,
I_{2}
, we have the desired bound. For the dense feature
\bm{M}_{j}^{\perp}
, the analysis is similar and we omit for brevity.
∎

Appendix K
Proof of Additional Lemmas

K.1
Proof of Lemma
H.1
:

Proof of Lemma
H.1
:

For the logit
\ell_{s,t}(\bm{X}_{n},\mathfrak{B})
of negative sample
\bm{X}_{n,s}
, we can simply calculate:

\displaystyle\left|\ell^{\prime}_{s,t}(\bm{X}_{n},\mathfrak{B})-\frac{1}{|\mathfrak{B}|}\right|

(432)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\frac{e^{\operatorname{Sim}_{f_{t}}(\bm{X}_{n},\bm{X}_{n,s})/\tau}}{\sum_{\bm{X}\in\mathfrak{B}}e^{\operatorname{Sim}_{f_{t}}(\bm{X}_{n},\bm{X})/\tau}}-\frac{1}{|\mathfrak{B}|}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{\bm{X}\in\mathfrak{B}}e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(\bm{X})-f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n,s})\rangle/\tau}\right)^{-1}-\frac{1}{|\mathfrak{B}|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left||\mathfrak{B}|-\sum_{\bm{X}\in\mathfrak{B}}e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(\bm{X})-f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n,s})\rangle/\tau}\right|\cdot\left(|\mathfrak{B}|\cdot\sum_{\bm{X}\in\mathfrak{B}}e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(\bm{X})-f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n,s})\rangle/\tau}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\bm{X}\in\mathfrak{B}}\left|1-e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(\bm{X})-f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n,s})\rangle/\tau}\right|\cdot\left(|\mathfrak{B}|\cdot\sum_{\bm{X}\in\mathfrak{B}}e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(\bm{X})-f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n,s})\rangle/\tau}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{\textcircled{\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{1}}}}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\max_{\bm{X}\in\mathfrak{B}}\left|\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(\bm{X})-f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n,s})\rangle\right|\cdot\left(\tau\sum_{\bm{X}\in\mathfrak{B}}e^{\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(\bm{X})-f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n,s})\rangle/\tau}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{\textcircled{\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{2}}}}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\sum_{i\in[m]}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}}{\tau|\mathfrak{B}|}\cdot\exp\left(\frac{\sum_{i\in[m]}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}}{\tau}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{\textcircled{\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{3}}}}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\sum_{i\in[m]}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}}{\tau|\mathfrak{B}|}\right)=\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{1}{\operatorname{polylog}(d)}\right).

\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{1}}⃝
is because we have
\left|1-e^{a}\right|\leq|a|\quad\text{for }a\leq 0.1
.

\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{2}}⃝
is because we have
\left|\left\langle f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n}),f_{t}(\bm{X})-f_{t}(\bm{X}_{n,s})\right\rangle\right|\leq\tilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\sum_{i\in[m]}\|\bm{w}_{i}^{(t)}\|_{2}^{2}\right)\cdot L^{2}

\raisebox{-0.3pt}{\tiny\ensuremath{3}}⃝
is because we have
e^{x}\leq O(1)\quad\text{for }x\leq\frac{1}{2}

∎

K.2
Proof of Lemma
J.1
:

Proof of Lemma
J.1
:

Recalling the definition of
\mathcal{M}_{j}
. According to Definition
B.1
and Lemma
B.2
(b), we know:

\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle^{2}\geq c_{2}\sigma_{0}^{2}\log d.

(433)

We can write:

\mathbb{P}[i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}\cap\mathcal{M}_{j^{\prime}}]=\mathbb{P}[i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}]\cdot\mathbb{P}[i\in\mathcal{M}_{j^{\prime}}].

(434)

Now,

\mathbb{P}[i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}]=\mathbb{P}[\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle^{2}\geq c_{2}\sigma_{0}^{2}\log d].

(435)

Since
\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma_{0}^{2})
, it follows that:

\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle^{2}/\sigma_{0}^{2}\sim\chi^{2}(1).

(436)

Using the tail bound of
\chi^{2}(1)
, we have:

\mathbb{P}\left[\langle\bm{w}_{i}^{(0)},\bm{M}_{j}\rangle^{2}\geq c_{2}\sigma_{0}^{2}\log d\right]=\mathbb{P}\left[\chi^{2}(1)\geq c_{2}\cdot\log d\right]\leq\exp\left(-\Omega(\log d)\right)=\frac{1}{d^{\Omega(1)}}.

(437)

Therefore:

\mathbb{P}[i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}\cap\mathcal{M}_{j^{\prime}}]\leq\frac{1}{d^{\Omega(1)}}.

(438)

There are
m
neurons in total, and each one falls into the intersection with probability
\frac{1}{d^{\Omega(1)}}
, so the total expectation is:

\mathbb{E}[|i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}\cap\mathcal{M}_{j^{\prime}}|]\leq m\cdot\frac{1}{d^{\Omega(1)}}.

(439)

It follows that
\mu=\mathbb{E}[|i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}\cap\mathcal{M}_{j^{\prime}}|]\leq O(\log d)
. We can use Chernoff Bound to derive high probability upper bound. First, we treat
X_{i}=\bm{1}_{i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}\cap\mathcal{M}_{j^{\prime}}}
as independent 0-1 Bernoulli variables and define:

X=\sum_{i=1}^{m}X_{i}=|i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}\cap\mathcal{M}_{j^{\prime}}|,\quad\text{and}\quad\mathbb{E}[X]=\mu\leq O(\log d).

(440)

Apply Chernoff bound we have:

\Pr[|i\in\mathcal{M}_{j}\cap\mathcal{M}_{j^{\prime}}|\leq O(\log d)]\geq 1-o(1/d^{4}).

(441)

∎
